|date=2012-11-15 |access-date=2012-11-15}}\n\n==Background==\nA bout between [[Erik Koch]] and [[Ricardo Lamas]], previously linked to [[UFC 155]], was moved to this event to bolster the main card.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/31492/erik-koch-vs-ricardo-lamas-featherweight-tilt-shifts-to-ufc-on-fox-6-in-chicago.mma|title=Erik Koch vs. Ricardo Lamas featherweight tilt shifts to UFC on FOX 6 in Chicago|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|date=2012-11-07|access-date=2012-11-11|author=Erickson, Matt|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20121110224948/http://mmajunkie.com/news/31492/erik-koch-vs-ricardo-lamas-featherweight-tilt-shifts-to-ufc-on-fox-6-in-chicago.mma|archive-date=2012-11-10}}\n\nBuddy Roberts was expected to face [[Michael Kuiper]] at the event. However, Roberts was forced out of the bout with an illness and was replaced by promotional newcomer Josh Janousek.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/01/josh-janousek-replaces-buddy-roberts-meets-michael-kuiper-at-ufc-on-fox-6|title=Josh Janousek replaces Buddy Roberts, meets Michael Kuiper at UFC on FOX 6|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|date=2013-01-15|access-date=2013-01-15|author=Staff|archive-date=2013-01-17|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130117160805/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/01/josh-janousek-replaces-buddy-roberts-meets-michael-kuiper-at-ufc-on-fox-6|url-status=dead}} Then just days before the event, Janousek himself pulled out of the bout citing an injury. With no time to find a suitable replacement for Kuiper, he was pulled from the event as well.{{cite web|url=http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2013/1/21/3900366/michael-kuiper-removed-from-ufc-on-fox-6-injury-josh-janousek-mma-news|title=Michael Kuiper removed from UFC on FOX 6 following injury to Josh Cedenblad]] was expected to face [[Rafael Natal]] at the event but was forced out of the bout with an injury and replaced by promotional newcomer [[Sean Spencer (fighter)|Sean Spencer]].{{cite reported to the [[Nevada State Athletic Commission]]. It does not include sponsor money and also does not include the UFC's traditional \"fight night\" bonuses or [[Pay-Per-View]] quotas.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/02/ufc-156-salaries-evans-overeem-and-aldo-earn-biggest-payouts|title=UFC 156 salaries: Evans, Overeem and Aldo earn biggest payouts|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|date=2013-02-04|access-date=2013-02-04|author=Staff|archive-date=2013-02-06|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130206213217/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/02/ufc-156-salaries-evans-overeem-and-aldo-earn-biggest-payouts|url-status=dead}}\n* '''José Aldo:''' $240,000 (includes $120,000 win bonus) def. Frankie Edgar: $120,000\n* '''Antônio Rogério Nogueira:''' $174,000 (includes $67,000 win bonus) def. Rashad Evans: $300,000\n* '''Antônio Silva:''' $70,000 (no win bonus) def. Alistair Overeem: $285,714\n* '''Demian Maia:''' $120,000 (includes $60,000 win bonus) def. Jon Fitch: $66,000\n* '''Joseph Benavidez:''' $60,000 (includes $30,000 win bonus) def. Ian McCall: $9,000\n* '''Evan Dunham:''' $46,000 (includes $23,000 win bonus) def. Gleison Tibau: $33,000\n* '''Tyron Woodley:''' $87,000 (includes $43,500 win bonus) def. Jay Hieron: $12,000\n* '''Bobby Green:''' $20,000 (includes $10,000 win bonus) def. Jacob Volkmann: $22,000\n* '''Isaac Vallie-Flagg:''' $20,000 (includes $10,000 win bonus) def. Yves Edwards: $21,000\n* '''Dustin Kimura:''' $16,000 (includes $8,000 win bonus) def. Chico Camus: $8,000 ^\n* '''Francisco Rivera:''' $16,000 (includes $8,000 win bonus) def. Edwin Figueroa: $10,000\n\n^ ''Dustin Kimura was reportedly fined 20 percent of his purse for failing to make the required weight for his fight. |archive-date=2011-06-27 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20110627142203/http://mmapayout.com/blue-book/pay-per-view/ |url-status=dead }}\n|previousevent=[[TUF 16 Finale|The Ultimate Fighter: Team Carwin vs. Team Nelson Finale]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC on FX: Belfort vs. Bisping]]\n|promotion=[[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]}}\n\n'''''UFC 155: dos Santos vs. Velasquez 2''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] on December 29, 2012, at the [[MGM Grand Garden Arena]] in [[Las Vegas Valley|Las Vegas]], [[Nevada]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/30942/ufc-155-headed-to-mgm-grand-garden-arena-in-las-vegas.mma|title=Year-end UFC 155 event headed to MGM Grand Garden Arena in Las Vegas|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|date=2012-10-04|access-date=2012-10-04|author=Staff|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://archive.today/20130128213436/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2012/10/ufc-155-headed-to-mgm-grand-garden-arena-in-las-vegas|archive-date=2013-01-28}}\n\n==Background==\nA [[Junior dos Santos]] vs. [[Cain Velasquez]] rematch had been expected since May 26, 2012 at [[UFC 146]], where dos Santos defended his Heavyweight championship against [[Frank Mir]] and Velasquez defeated [[Antônio Silva (fighter)|Antônio Silva]]. Velasquez and dos Santos had already fought once on November 12, 2011 at [[UFC on Fox: Velasquez vs. Dos Santos]], where dos Santos won via knockout in the first round. [[Alistair Overeem]], former [[Dream (mixed martial arts)|DREAM]] and [[Strikeforce (mixed martial arts)|Strikeforce]] champion, was expected to face the winner of this bout. He was originally expected to face dos Santos for the title at UFC 146; however, on April 4, 2012, Overeem was revealed to have failed his pre-fight drug test by the [[Nevada State Athletic Commission]] (NSAC). He was found to have an elevated [[testosterone (medication)|testosterone]] to [[epitestosterone]] ratio of 14-to-1, over the allowed ratio rate of 6-to-1.{{cite web|url=http://sportv.globo.com/site/eventos/combate/noticia/2012/10/white-confirma-cigano-x-velasquez-no-ufc-155-overeem-pega-o-vencedor.html|title=White confirms Cigano x Velasquez at UFC 155; Overeem takes the winner|publisher=Globo.com|access-date=2012-11-06}}{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/28109/alistair-overeem-fails-pre-fight-drug-test-likely-out-of-ufc-146-title-fight.mma|title=Alistair Overeem fails pre-fight drug test, likely out of UFC 146 title fight|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|date=2012-04-04|access-date=2012-11-06|author=Marrocco, Steven|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20120404213449/http://mmajunkie.com/news/28109/alistair-overeem-fails-pre-fight-drug-test-likely-out-of-ufc-146-title-fight.mma|archive-date=2012-04-04}}\n\n[[Chael Sonnen]] was expected to face [[Forrest Griffin]] at this event. However, Sonnen was pulled from the fight to coach the seventeenth season of ''[[The Ultimate Fighter]]'' opposite UFC Light Heavyweight champion [[Jon Jones]] and was replaced by [[Phil Davis (fighter)|Phil Davis]].{{cite web|url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Phil-Davis-Replaces-Chael-Sonnen-Meets-Forrest-Griffin-at-UFC-155-on-Dec-29-in-Las-Vegas-46641|title=Phil Davis Replaces Chael Sonnen, Meets Forrest Griffin at UFC 155 on Dec. 29 in Las Vegas|publisher=Sherdog.com|date=2012-10-19|access-date=2012-10-20|author=Whitman, Mike}} Griffin was then forced out of the bout with a knee injury and Davis was pulled from the card altogether.\n\nA bout between [[Erik Koch]] and [[Ricardo Lamas]] was expected to take place at this event. However, the bout was moved to [[UFC on Fox 6]] and promoted to the main card.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/31492/erik-koch-vs-ricardo-lamas-featherweight-tilt-shifts-to-ufc-on-fox-6-in-chicago.mma|title=Erik Koch vs. Ricardo Lamas featherweight tilt shifts to UFC on FOX 6 in Chicago|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|date=2012-11-07|access-date=2012-11-11|author=Erickson, Matt|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20121110224948/http://mmajunkie.com/news/31492/erik-koch-vs-ricardo-lamas-featherweight-tilt-shifts-to-ufc-on-fox-6-in-chicago.mma|archive-date=2012-11-10}}\n\n[[Gray Maynard]] was expected to face [[Joe Lauzon]] at this event. However, Maynard was forced out of the bout with an injury and replaced by [[Jim Miller (fighter)|Jim Miller]].{{cite web |url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2012/11/gray-maynard-injured-jim-miller-steps-in-to-face-joe-lauzon-at-ufc-155 |title=Gray Maynard injured, Jim Miller steps in to face Joe Lauzon at UFC 155 | News – |publisher=Mmajunkie.com |date=2012-11-14 |access-date=2012-12-30 |archive-date=2012-11-29 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20121129223846/http://mmajunkie.com/news/2012/11/gray-maynard-injured-jim-miller-steps-in-to-face-joe-lauzon-at-ufc-155 |url-status=dead }}\n\n[[Matt Mitrione]] was expected to face [[Phil De Fries]] at this event. However, due to an injury sustained by [[Shane Carwin]], Mitrione stepped in as a replacement against [[Roy Nelson (fighter)|Roy Nelson]] at ''[[The Ultimate Fighter 16 Finale]]''{{cite web |url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2012/11/matt-mitrione-replaces-shane-carwin-meets-roy-nelson-at-tuf-16-finale |title=Matt Mitrione replaces Shane Carwin, meets Roy Nelson at TUF 16 Finale | News – |publisher=Mmajunkie.com |date=2012-11-15 |access-date=2012-12-30 |archive-date=2012-12-02 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20121202032755/http://mmajunkie.com/news/2012/11/matt-mitrione-replaces-shane-carwin-meets-roy-nelson-at-tuf-16-finale |url-status=dead }} and was replaced by the returning [[Todd Duffee]].{{cite web |url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2012/11/todd-duffee-back-in-the-ufc-faces-phil-de-fries-at-ufc-155 |title=Todd Duffee back in the UFC, faces Phil De Fries at UFC 155 | News – |publisher=Mmajunkie.com |date=2012-11-21 |access-date=2012-12-30 |archive-date=2012-12-25 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20121225104542/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2012/11/todd-duffee-back-in-the-ufc-faces-phil-de-fries-at-ufc-155 |url-status=dead }}\n\n[[Chris Weidman]] was expected to face [[Tim Boetsch]] at this event. Hettes]] was expected to face [[Steven Siler]] at the event; however, Hettes was forced out of the bout with an injury and replaced by Kurt Holobaugh.{{cite Proctor]] was expected to face [[Al Iaquinta]] at the event. However, the bout was scrapped as both fighters sustained training injuries leading up to the fight.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/04/al-iaquinta-also-injured-fight-pulled-from-ufc-159-lineup|title=Al Iaquinta also injured, fight pulled from UFC 159 lineup|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-04-12|access-date=2013-04-13|archive-date=2013-04-14|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130414001955/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/04/al-iaquinta-also-injured-fight-pulled-from-ufc-159-lineup|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Johnny Bedford]] was expected to face [[Erik Pérez]] at the event. However, at the weigh ins Catone missed weight and the bout was subsequently pulled from the card the day of the show after Catone went to the hospital for dehydration. The bout was scrapped as a result.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/04/nick-catone-vs-james-head-pulled-from-tonights-ufc-159-lineup|title=Nick Catone vs. James Head pulled from Ultimate Fighter: Brazil 2#The Ultimate Fighter: Brazil 2 Finale|UFC on Fuel TV: Nogueira vs. Werdum]]\n}}\n'''''UFC 160: Velasquez vs. Bigfoot 2''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held on May 25, 2013, at the [[MGM Grand Garden Arena]] in [[Las Vegas, Nevada]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/02/ufc-160-slated-for-may-25-in-las-vegas-on-memorial-day-weekend|title=UFC 160 slated for May 25 in Las Vegas on Memorial Day weekend|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-02-11|access-date=2013-02-11|archive-date=2017-08-16|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170816013512/http://mmajunkie.com/news/2013/02/ufc-160-slated-for-may-25-in-las-vegas-on-memorial-day-weekend|url-status=dead}}\n\n==Background==\nThe main event was a rematch between former [[UFC Heavyweight Championship|UFC Heavyweight Champion]] [[Cain Velasquez]] and [[Antônio Silva (fighter)|Antônio Silva]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmamania.com/2013/2/12/3979250/cain-velasquez-vs-bigfoot-silva-rematch-ufc-160-main-event-las-vegas-mma|title=Cain Velasquez vs 'Bigfoot' Silva rematch to serve as UFC 160 main event on May 25 in Las Vegas|publisher=MMAmania.com|last=Guillen|first=Adam Jr.|date=2013-02-12|access-date=2013-02-12}} In their first encounter at [[UFC 146]] a year earlier, Velasquez defeated Silva via first-round TKO.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2012/05/ufc-146-results-antonio-silva-vs-cain-velasquez|title=UFC 146 results: Cain Velasquez batters and bloodies Antonio Silva for TKO win|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|last=Stupp|first=Dann|date=2012-05-26|access-date=2012-05-26|archive-date=2012-12-26|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20121226023320/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2012/05/ufc-146-results-antonio-silva-vs-cain-velasquez|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Alistair Overeem]] was expected to face [[Junior dos Santos]] at the event. However, in early March, Overeem pulled out of the bout citing a leg injury.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2013/3/6/4072916/alistair-overeem-injured-out-of-ufc-160-fight-against-junior-dos|title=Alistair Overeem injured, out of UFC 160 fight against Junior dos Santos|publisher=MMAfighting.com|last=Helwani|first=Ariel|date=2013-03-06|access-date=2012-03-06}} It was initially announced that the bout would be rescheduled for a future event, later in the summer of this year.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/03/after-ufc-160-scratch-alistair-overeem-vs-jds-now-likely-in-summer|title=After UFC 160 scratch, Alistair Overeem vs. JDS now likely in summer|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-03-07|access-date=2012-03-07|archive-date=2013-03-09|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130309205638/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/03/after-ufc-160-scratch-alistair-overeem-vs-jds-now-likely-in-summer|url-status=dead}} Then, on March 9 it was announced that Dos Santos would remain on the card and face [[Mark Hunt]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2013/3/9/4082300/mark-hunt-vs-junior-dos-santos-on-for-ufc-160|title=Mark Hunt vs. Junior dos Santos on for UFC 160|publisher=MMAFighting.com|last=Helwani|first=Ariel|date=2013-03-09|access-date=2012-03-09}}\n\n[[Ryan Bader]] was expected to face [[Glover Teixeira]] at the event but withdrew from the bout due to injury in March. He was replaced by [[James Te Huna]]{{cite web|url=http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2013/3/11/4092178/mma-news-ryan-bader-injured-james-te-huna-fights-glover-teixeira-ufc-160|title=Ryan Bader injured, James Te Huna fights Glover Teixeira at UFC 160|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|last=Alexander|first=Mookie|date=2013-03-11|access-date=2013-03-11|archive-date=2013-03-15|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130315001532/http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2013/3/11/4092178/mma-news-ryan-bader-injured-james-te-huna-fights-glover-teixeira-ufc-160|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Amir Sadollah]] was expected to face [[Stephen Thompson (fighter)|Stephen Thompson]] at the event. However, Sadollah pulled out of the bout citing an injury and was replaced by [[Nah-Shon Burrell]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/04/amir-sadollah-injured-nah-shon-burrell-now-meets-stephen-thompson-at-ufc-160|title=Amir Sadollah injured, Nah-Shon Burrell now meets Stephen Thompson at UFC 160|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-04-03|access-date=2013-04-03|archive-date=2013-04-06|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130406020623/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/04/amir-sadollah-injured-nah-shon-burrell-now-meets-stephen-thompson-at-ufc-160|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Gunnar Nelson (fighter)|Gunnar Nelson]] was expected to face [[Mike Pyle (fighter)|Mike Pyle]] at the event. However, Nelson pulled out of the bout citing an injury and was replaced by [[Rick Story]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/04/rick-story-replaces-gunnar-nelson-meets-mike-pyle-at-ufc-160|title=Rick Story replaces Gunnar Nelson, meets Mike Pyle at UFC 160|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-04-10|access-date=2013-04-10|archive-date=2013-04-13|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130413010946/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/04/rick-story-replaces-gunnar-nelson-meets-mike-pyle-at-ufc-160|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Dana White]] announced at the [[UFC on Fox 7]] post-fight press conference that he expects that the winner of the [[Gray Maynard]]/[[T. J. Grant]] fight would get a UFC Lightweight title shot against [[Benson Henderson]].{{cite web|url=http://topmmanews.com/2013/04/20/dana-white-announces-tj-grantmaynard-winner-to-fight-for-ufc-lightweight-title//|title=Dana White Announces T. J. Grant/Maynard Winner to Fight for UFC Lightweight Title |work=TopMMANews.com|date=April 20, 2013|last=Grienke |first=Keith |access-date=April 20, 2013}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, [[Khabib Nurmagomedov]] came in heavy weighing in at 158.5 lb. Nurmagomedov was given two hours to cut to the lightweight maximum of 156 pounds, but he elected instead to surrender a percentage of his fight purse to his opponent [[Abel Trujillo]] and the bout was contested at a catchweight.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/05/ufc-160-live-and-official-weigh-in-results|title=Official UFC 160 weigh-in results|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|date=2013-05-24|access-date=2010-05-24|archive-date=2013-07-09|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130709020225/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/05/ufc-160-live-and-official-weigh-in-results|url-status=dead}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Heavyweight\n|[[Cain Velasquez]] (c)\n|def.\n|[[Antônio Silva (fighter)|Antônio Silva]]\n|TKO (punches)\n|1\n|1:21\n|For the [[List of UFC Champions#Heavyweight Championship|UFC Heavyweight Championship]].\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Heavyweight\n|[[Junior dos Santos]]\n|def.\n|[[Mark Hunt]]\n| KO (spinning hook kick)\n|3\n|4:18\n| \n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Light Heavyweight\n|[[Glover Teixeira]]\n|def.\n|[[James Te Huna]]\n|Submission (guillotine choke)\n|1\n|2:38\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight\n|[[T. |url=http://www.ufc.com/event/UFC160#/fight|title=UFC® 160: Velasquez vs. Silva II|publisher=Ultimate Fighting Championship|access-date=2013-02-18}}\n\n==Bonus awards==\nThe following fighters received $50,000 bonuses.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/05/ufc-160-bonuses-earn-fight-night-awards|title=UFC 160 bonuses: dos Santos, Hunt, Grant, Teixeira earn $50,000|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|date=2012-05-26|access-date=2013-05-26|author=Staff|archive-date=2013-10-05|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20131005083432/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/05/ufc-160-bonuses-earn-fight-night-awards|url-status=dead}}\n\n* '''Fight of the Night: Junior Dos Santos vs. Mark Hunt'''\n* '''Knockout of the Night: T. J. Grant'''\n* '''Submission of the Night: Glover Teixeira'''\n\n==Reported payout==\n\nThe following is the reported payout to the fighters as reported to the [[Nevada State Athletic Commission]]. It does not include sponsor money and also does not include the UFC's traditional \"fight night\" bonuses.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/05/ufc-160-salaries-velasquez-and-dos-santos-are-top-earners-lead-payroll|title=UFC 160 salaries: Velasquez and dos Santos are top earners, lead payroll|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|date=2013-05-28|access-date=2013-05-28|author=Staff|archive-date=2013-06-07|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130607120756/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/05/ufc-160-salaries-velasquez-and-dos-santos-are-top-earners-lead-payroll|url-status=dead}}\n* '''Cain Velasquez:''' $400,000 (no win bonus) def. Antônio Silva: $75,000\n* '''Junior dos Santos:''' $240,000 (includes $120,000 win bonus) def. Mark Hunt: $160,000\n* '''Glover Teixeira:''' $48,000 (includes $24,000 win bonus) def. James Te-Huna: $30,000\n* '''T. J. Grant:''' $50,000 (includes $25,000 win bonus) def. Gray Maynard: $45,000\n* '''Donald Cerrone:''' $82,000 (includes $41,000 win bonus) def. K. J. Noons: $41,000\n* '''Mike Pyle:''' $84,000 (includes $42,000 win bonus) def. Rick Story: $27,000\n* '''Dennis Bermudez:''' $28,000 (includes $14,000 win bonus) def. Max Holloway: $14,000\n* '''Robert Whittaker:''' $30,000 (includes $15,000 win bonus) def. Colton Smith: $15,000\n* '''Khabib Nurmagomedov:''' $28,000 (includes $14,000 win bonus) def. Abel Trujillo: $8,000 ^\n* '''Stephen Thompson:''' $16,000 (includes $8,000 win bonus) def. Nah-Shon Burrell: $9,000\n* '''George Roop:''' $26,000 (includes $13,000 win bonus ) def. Brian Bowles: $19,000\n* '''Jeremy Stephens:''' $48,000 (includes $24,000 win bonus) def. Estevan Payan: $10,000\n\n^ ''Khabib Nurmagomedov was reportedly fined 20 percent of his purse for failing to make the required weight for his fight. The Nevada State Athletic Commission's initial report did not include information on the penalty.''\n\n==See also==\n*[[List of UFC events]]\n*[[2013 in Bisping==\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=UFC on FX: Belfort vs. Bisping\n|image=UFC_on_FX_Belfort_vs._Bisping_poster.jpg\n|date=January 19, 2013\n|venue=[[Ginásio do Ibirapuera]]\n|city=[[São Paulo|São Paulo, Brazil]]\n|attendance=9,116\n|previousevent=[[UFC 155#UFC 155: dos Santos vs. Velasquez 2|UFC 155: dos Santos vs. Velasquez 2]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC on Fox: Johnson vs. Dodson]]\n|promotion=[[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]}}\n\n'''''UFC on FX: Belfort vs. Bisping''''' (also known as '''''UFC on FX 7''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] on January 19, 2013, at [[Ginásio do Ibirapuera]] in [[São Paulo|São Paulo, Brazil]].{{cite web |url=http://www.ufc.com/event/FX7#/fight |title=UFC on FX: Belfort vs.Bisping |publisher=Ultimate Fighting Championship| date=2012-11-21 |access-date=2012-11-21}}{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/31435/ufcs-return-to-brazil-on-jan-19-will-be-ufc-on-fx-7.mma |title=UFC's Jan. 19 return to Brazil will be UFC on FX 7 |last=Erickson |first=Matt |date=2012-11-03 |access-date=2012-11-03 |publisher=MMAjunkie.com |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20121106094702/http://mmajunkie.com/news/31435/ufcs-return-to-brazil-on-jan-19-will-be-ufc-on-fx-7.mma |archive-date=2012-11-06 }}\n\n===Background===\nThe main fight featured middleweights [[Michael Bisping]] and [[Vitor Belfort]]. The event also held the first UFC fight for [[Daniel Sarafian]], middleweight finalist on the first season of [[The Ultimate Fighter: Brazil]]. Sarafian was injured before the championship fight with [[Cezar Ferreira]], who went on to win the show.{{cite web |url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2012/11/3/3595226/bisping-vs-belfort-will-headline-ufcs-jan-19-show-in-brazil |title=Bisping vs. Belfort will headline UFC's Jan. 19 show in Brazil |last=Meltzer |first=Dave |date=2012-11-03 |access-date=2012-11-22 |publisher=MMAFighting.com}}\n\n[[Johnny Eduardo]] was briefly linked to a bout at this event against [[Iuri Alcântara]], but was replaced by [[George Roop]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/31407/report-johnny-eduardo-out-george-roop-in-vs-yuri-alcantara-at-ufcs-return-to-brazil.mma |title=Report: Johnny Eduardo out, George Roop in vs. Yuri Alcantara at UFC's return to Brazil |publisher=MMAjunkie.com |last=Erickson |first=Matt |date=2012-11-02 |access-date=2012-11-02 |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20121104050712/http://mmajunkie.com/news/31407/report-johnny-eduardo-out-george-roop-in-vs-yuri-alcantara-at-ufcs-return-to-brazil.mma |archive-date=2012-11-04 }} Roop was then injured and replaced by UFC newcomer Pedro Nobre.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/01/report-pedro-nobre-replaces-george-roop-meets-yuri-alcantara-at-ufc-on-fx-7 |title=Report: Report: Pedro Nobre replaces George Roop, meets Yuri Alcantara at UFC on FX 7 |publisher=MMAjunkie.com |date=2013-01-06 |access-date=2013-01-06 |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130108111632/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/01/report-pedro-nobre-replaces-george-roop-meets-yuri-alcantara-at-ufc-on-fx-7 |archive-date=2013-01-08 }}\n\n[[Thiago Perpetuo]] was expected to face [[Michael Kuiper]] at the event; however, Perpetuo suffered an injury and was replaced by [[Caio Magalhaes]].{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/2012/12/caio-magalhaes-replaces-thiago-perpetuo-faces-michael-kuiper-at-ufc-on-fx-7|title=Caio Magalhaes replaces Thiago Perpetuo, faces Michael Kuiper at UFC on FX 7|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2012-12-01|access-date=2012-12-01}} On December 27, it was announced that Magalhaes had also pulled out of the bout and that Kuiper would be shifted to [[UFC on Fox 6]] on January 26, 2013 to face Buddy Roberts.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2012/12/kuiper-magalhaes-off-ufc-on-fx-7-kuiper-roberts-joins-ufc-on-fox-6|title=Kuiper-Magalhaes off UFC on FX 7, Kuiper-Roberts joins UFC on FOX 6|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2012-12-27|access-date=2012-12-27|archive-date=2012-12-29|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20121229141001/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2012/12/kuiper-magalhaes-off-ufc-on-fx-7-kuiper-roberts-joins-ufc-on-fox-6|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Roger Hollett]] was expected to face [[Wagner Prado]] at the event; however, Hollett was forced out of the bout with a torn bicep and was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Ildemar Alcântara]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/01/report-roger-hollett-out-wagner-prado-meets-ildemar-alcantara-at-ufc-on-fx-7|title=Roger Hollett out, Wagner Prado meets Ildemar Alcantara at UFC on FX 7|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-01-08|access-date=2013-01-08|archive-date=2013-01-10|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130110184038/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/01/report-roger-hollett-out-wagner-prado-meets-ildemar-alcantara-at-ufc-on-fx-7|url-status=dead}}\n\n===Results===\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (FX)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Middleweight\n|[[Vitor Belfort]]\n|def.\n|[[Michael Bisping]]\n|TKO (head kick and punches)\n|2\n|1:27\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Middleweight\n|[[C. Jones vs. Sonnen]]\n|promotion=[[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]}}\n'''''UFC on Fox: Henderson vs. Melendez''''' (also known as '''''UFC on Fox 7''''') was a mixed martial arts event held on April 20, 2013, at the [[HP Pavilion at San Jose|HP Pavilion]] in [[San Jose, California]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/01/ufc-targets-hp-pavilion-in-san-jose-for-april-20-ufc-on-fox-7-card|title=UFC targets HP Pavilion in San Jose for April 20 UFC on FOX 7 card|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|date=2013-01-02|access-date=2013-01-02}} The event was broadcast live on [[Fox Broadcasting Company|FOX]].\n\n===Background===\nThe event was headlined by a [[UFC Lightweight Championship]] bout between defending champion [[Benson Henderson]] and [[List of Strikeforce champions|Strikeforce Lightweight Champion]] [[Gilbert Melendez]].{{cite web |url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2013/1/14/3875970/ufc-on-fox-7-targeted-for-benson-henderson-vs-gilbert-melendez |title=UFC on FOX 7 targeted for Benson Henderson vs. Gilbert Melendez lightweight title fight |publisher=mmafighting.com |author=Mike Chiapetta|date=2013-01-14|access-date=2013-01-14}} Also featured on the card was a bout between two time [[UFC Heavyweight Championship|UFC Heavyweight Champion]] [[Frank Mir]] and [[List of Strikeforce champions#Tournaments|Strikeforce Heavyweight Grand Prix Champion]] [[Daniel Cormier]].{{cite web |url=http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2013/1/15/3879822/mma-news-frank-mir-vs-daniel-cormier-ufc-on-fox-7 |title=Frank Mir vs. Daniel Cormier set for UFC on FOX 7 in San Jose |publisher=bloodyelbow.com |author=Mookie Alexander |date=2013-01-15 |access-date=2013-01-15 |archive-date=2013-01-18 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130118032222/http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2013/1/15/3879822/mma-news-frank-mir-vs-daniel-cormier-ufc-on-fox-7 |url-status=dead }}\n\n[[Clay Guida]] was expected to face [[Chad Mendes]] at this event. However it was revealed on March 15 that Guida had pulled out of the bout citing an injury{{cite web |url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Clay-Guida-Injured-Out-of-Chad-Mendes-Fight-at-UFC-on-Fox-7-50925 |title=Clay Guida Injured, Out of Chad Mendes Fight at UFC on Fox 7 |publisher=sherdog.com |author=Greg Savage|date=2013-03-15|access-date=2013-03-15}} and was replaced by [[Darren Elkins]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/03/darren-elkins-steps-in-for-clay-guida-meets-chad-mendes-at-ufc-on-fox-7 |title=Darren Elkins steps in for Clay Guida, meets Chad Mendes at UFC on FOX 7 |publisher=mmajunkie.com |author=Matt Erickson |date=2013-03-19 |access-date=2013-03-19 |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130321102551/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/03/darren-elkins-steps-in-for-clay-guida-meets-chad-mendes-at-ufc-on-fox-7 |archive-date=2013-03-21 }}\n\n[[Dan Hardy]] was expected to face [[Matt Brown (fighter)|Matt Brown]] at the event. However, Hardy was forced out of the bout due to medical issues regarding his heart and was replaced by [[Jordan Mein]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/03/injury-forces-dan-hardy-off-ufc-on-fox-7-jordan-mein-now-meets-matt-brown |title=Injury forces Dan Hardy off UFC on FOX 7; Jordan Mein now meets Matt Brown |publisher=mmajunkie.com |author=Matt Erickson |date=2013-03-22 |access-date=2013-03-22 |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130324015238/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/03/injury-forces-dan-hardy-off-ufc-on-fox-7-jordan-mein-now-meets-matt-brown |archive-date=2013-03-24 }}\n\n[[Francisco Rivera (fighter)|Francisco Rivera]] was expected to face [[Hugo Viana (fighter)|Hugo Viana]] at the event. However, Rivera was forced out of the bout with an injury and replaced by [[T.J. Dillashaw]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/03/t-j-dillashaw-replaces-francisco-rivera-meets-hugo-viana-at-ufc-on-fox-7 |title=T.J. (UFC)-produced [[reality television]] series ''[[The Ultimate Fighter]]''.{{cite web |url=http://www.theultimatefighter.com/news/story/1694196 |title=TUF 17 Face/Off |work=theultimatefighter.com |date=January 9, 2013 |access-date=January 9, 2013}}\n\nThe series was officially announced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship|UFC]] in October 2012 with [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship|Light Heavyweight Champion]] [[Jon Jones]] coaching opposite [[Chael Sonnen]].{{cite web |url=https://www.usatoday.com/story/sports/mma/2012/10/16/jon-jones-chael-sonnen-will-coach-tuf-fighters-then-meet/1637199/ |title=Jones, Sonnen will coach 'TUF' fighters, then meet |publisher=USAtoday.com |date=December 3, 2012 |access-date=January 9, 2013}} The two coaches met in a title fight on April 27, 2013 at [[UFC 159]]. This move drew criticism from [[Mixed martial arts|MMA]] critics and fans, as many felt that Sonnen, who had previously lost his last match and not competed in the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship|UFC]] [[Light heavyweight (MMA)|Light Heavyweight]] division in years, had not done enough to earn a title shot.{{cite web |url=https://www.usatoday.com/story/sports/mma/2012/10/16/jon-jones-and-chael-sonnen-on-tuf-brilliant-and-ridiculous/1637685/ |title=Jones and Sonnen on 'TUF' both brilliant and ridiculous |publisher=USAtoday.com |date=October 16, 2012 |access-date=January 9, 2013}}{{cite web |url=https://www.espn.com/blog/mma/post/_/id/14817/jonessonnen-pairing-is-a-shticky-situation |url=https://sports.yahoo.com/news/why-does-chael-sonnen-fight-061951108--mma.html |title=Why Does Chael Sonnen Get to Fight Jon Jones? |publisher=Yahoo.com |date=October 18, 2012 |access-date=January 9, 2013}}{{cite web |url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2012/10/the-sunday-junkie-oct-21-edition |title=The Sunday Junkie: Fans React to Jones/Sonnen |publisher=MMAjunkie.com |date=October 21, 2012 |access-date=January 9, 2013}}\n\nThis season was the first to air on Tuesdays, after [[The Ultimate Fighter: Live]] and [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Carwin vs. Team Nelson]]'s failure to generate the desired ratings for [[FX (TV channel)|FX]] on Friday evenings.{{cite web |url=https://www.usatoday.com/story/sports/mma/2012/12/03/the-ultimate-fighter-tuesdays-jon-jones-chael-sonnen/1744619/ [[Zak Cummings]], [[Tor Troéng]], Jimmy Quinlan, [[Kevin Casey (fighter)|Kevin Casey]], [[Kelvin Gastelum]]\n*Team Jones\n**[[Clint Hester]], [[Josh Samman]], [[Robert McDaniel|Robert \"Bubba\" McDaniel]], Gilbert Smith, Collin Hart, Adam Cella, [[Dylan Andrews]]\n\n; Fighters eliminated during the entry round\n*Kito Andrews, Lou Bercier, Ryan Bigler, Andy Enz, Nik Fekete, Jake Heun, Mike Jasper, Nicholas Kohring, Fraser Opie, Mike Persons, Scott Rosa, Eldon Sproat, Eric Wahlin, Tim Williams\n\n==Episodes==\n\n;'''Episode 1: \"Enter the Octagon\" (January 22, 2013)'''\n*Dana White greeted the head coaches, Jon Jones and Chael Sonnen, in the Ultimate Fighter training center.\n*Unlike previous seasons, the 28 middleweights were allowed to bring family and friends to watch their preliminary fights held in order to see who got into the house.\n*The preliminary fights began:\n\n:Adam Cella defeated Jake Heun via submission (armbar) in the first round.\n:Zak Cummings defeated Nik Fekete by KO (punches) in the first round.\n:Kevin Casey defeated Eldon Sproat via submission (rear naked choke).\n:Tor Troeng defeated Scott Rosa via submission (rear naked choke).\n:Clint Hester defeated Fraser Opie by decision.\n:Bubba McDaniel defeated Ryan Bigler via TKO (punches) in the second round.\n:Josh Samman defeated Lou Bercier via TKO (punches) in the first round.\n:Kelvin Gastelum defeated Kito Andrews by decision.\n:Jimmy Quinlan defeated Mike Persons via TKO (punches) in the first round.\n:Uriah Hall defeated Andy Enz by decision.\n:Gilbert Smith defeated Eric Wahlin via submission (arm triangle choke)\n:Luke Barnatt defeated Nicholas Kohring by decision.\n:Dylan Andrews defeated Tim Williams by decision.\n:Collin Hart defeated Mike Jasper via submission (rear naked choke) in the first round.\n\n*White announced that there would be a $5,000 bonus per finish during the tournament; additionally, fighters could receive $25,000 for ''Knockout of the Season'', ''Submission of the Season'' and ''Fight of the Season''.\n*Team Jones and Team Sonnen then picked teams and White flipped a coin (red for Jones, black for Sonnen). Sonnen won the coin toss and opted to choose the first fighter.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/01/episode-no-1-recap-the-ultimate-fighter-17-team-jones-vs-team-sonnen|title=Episode No. 1 recap: 'The Ultimate Fighter 17: Team Jones vs. Team Sonnen'|date=2013-01-22|publisher=mmajunkie.com}} The fighters were picked in the following order:\n\n{| class=\"wikitable\"\n|-\n!Coach\n!1st Pick\n!2nd Pick\n!3rd Pick\n!4th Pick\n!5th Pick\n!6th Pick\n!7th Pick\n|-\n!Sonnen\n|Luke Barnatt\n|Uriah Hall\n|Zak Cummings\n|Tor Troeng\n|Jimmy Quinlan\n|Kevin Casey\n|Kelvin Gastelum\n|-\n!Jones\n|Clint Hester\n|Josh Samman\n|Bubba McDaniel\n|Gilbert Smith\n|Collin Hart\n|Adam Cella\n|Dylan Andrews\n|}\n*Jones then matched Smith against Barnatt, feeling that the much shorter fighter taking out Sonnen's first pick would boost a lot of morale for his team.\n\n;'''Episode 2: \"Ambush\" (January 29, 2013)'''{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/01/episode-no-2-recap-the-ultimate-fighter-17-team-jones-vs-team-sonnen|title=Episode No. 2 recap: 'The Ultimate Fighter 17: Team Jones vs. Team Sonnen' - MMAjunkie|work=MMAjunkie}}\n*The red team voiced their displeasure with Jones' choice to match the 5'9\" Gilbert Smith against the 6'6\" Luke Barnatt.\n*On the ride back to the house, Smith talked about the lack of positive feedback he received during training. After they reached the house, he proceeded to cry when thinking about his family.\n*Team Jones called out Smith in the locker room about his emotional condition which left him upset. Jones then entered and reaffirmed his faith in Smith.\n*Sonnen visited his team at the house. He took Uriah Hall aside and told him he would rather that he get an easy fight for his preliminary bout. However, Hall disagreed and stated he rather fight the top picks from the opposing team.\n*Luke Barnatt defeated Gilbert Smith via KO (flying knee) in the second round.\n*Immediately after the fight, Bubba McDaniel called out Kevin Casey who felt disrespected by the sudden call out.\n*With the black team now in control, Sonnen selected Hall to fight Team Jones' Adam Cella.\n\n;'''Episode 3: \"Embrace the Suffering\" (February 5, 2013)'''{{cite web|url=http://www.tvguide.com/detail/tv-show.aspx?tvobjectid=191866&more=ucepisodelist&episodeid=21499389|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20160305225528/http://www.tvguide.com/tvshows/the-ultimate-fighter/episode-3-season-17/embrace-the-suffering/191866/|url-status=dead|archive-date=March 5, 2016|title=Watch The Ultimate Fighter Season 17 Episode 3: Embrace the Suffering - TVGuide.com|work=TVGuide.com}}{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/02/episode-no-3-recap-the-ultimate-fighter-17-team-jones-vs-team-sonnen-z|title=Episode No. 3 recap: 'The Ultimate Fighter 17: Team Jones vs. Team Sonnen' - MMAjunkie|work=MMAjunkie}}\n*On the ride back to the house, Kevin Casey continued to voice his displeasure with Bubba McDaniel's call out of him. He explained to his team that he still had a cut healing on his forehead.\n*Back at the house, Uriah Hall mentioned to the other members of the house that he had heard that Tor Troeng was a \"professional cooker,\" in which Josh Samman then corrected him by saying that the term is \"chef.\" Hall was then angered about the tone in which Samman corrected him with which left him claiming that he would call out Samman next. He then proceeded to talk about the times he was bullied as a teen.\n*After a training session, Hall met with Sonnen in a one-on-one to talk about his inner demons. Sonnen proceeded to explain to Hall about his own similar situation and on how he dealt with his demons.\n*On the ride to the gym, Casey told his team that he rather fight McDaniel at a later time. His teammate Jimmy Quinlan then proceeded to tease him for being scared.\n*Before the second preliminary match, Casey's picture was already hung on the tournament bracket signaling that he would fight next. McDaniel proceeded to hang his own picture on the bracket to bait Team Sonnen into matching them up.\n*Jones visited his team at the house, bringing along food to grill. Each member of the team then talked about what being in the UFC would mean to them.\n*Uriah Hall defeated Adam Cella via KO (spinning hook kick) in first round.\n*After the knockout, Cella remained unconscious on the canvas for quite some time. He eventually regained consciousness and was taken to the hospital.\n*In the locker room, Team Sonnen remarked on how Hall pretty much sealed the deal for getting the \"Knockout of the Season\" bonus. White entered shortly after and remarked the same thing.\n*With Sonnen's team still in control, Casey was matched up against Team Jones' Collin Hart, much to the dismay of McDaniel.\n\n;'''Episode 4: \"Sign of Disrespect\" (February 12, 2013)'''\n*Kevin Casey, Gilbert Smith, and Clint Hester had a freestyle rap battle in the house.\n*During a power outage in the house, Team Sonnen decided to prank the red team by wrapping them up in toilet paper while they were sleeping. However, the joke was unsuccessful when they just threw the rolls of paper into Team Jones' rooms and ran.\n*During the weigh-ins, Hart [[The finger|flipped off]] Casey, which led to an intense staredown between the two.\n*Taken by surprise by Hart's actions, Uriah Hall confronted him at the house and asked why he was disrespectful towards Casey and Team Sonnen. Hart replies that he was in the moment of television and was upset that Casey disturbed his sleep.\n*Collin Hart defeated Kevin Casey via unanimous decision.\n*Now with Jones' team back in control, he announced the next fight: Bubba McDaniel vs. Kelvin Gastelum. At 21 years old, Gastelum is the youngest fighter in the history of the show.\n\n;'''Episode 5: \"The Reflection of Perfection\" (February 19, 2013)'''\n*Sonnen visited the house and they played a game of [[charades]]. However, Bubba McDaniel, who is focused on his fight, decides not to join the game.\n*Josh Samman begins to emerge as Team Jones' unofficial captain.\n*Samman informed the coaches that he preferred to fight later since he had some injuries. Jones was receptive but the other coaches felt Samman was taking too much control over the team.\n*Actor [[Mickey Rourke]] visited the Team Sonnen fighters at the house to share insights from his days when he was a boxer.\n*Sonnen received a call from UFC women's bantamweight champion [[Ronda Rousey]], who wished Kelvin Gastelum good luck on his fight and said that if he won, she would come down to the TUF gym and teach a session.\n*Kelvin Gastelum defeated Bubba McDaniel via submission (rear naked choke) in the second round.\n*Now with Sonnen's team back in control, he matched Tor Troeng against Samman.\n\n;'''Episode 6: \"Path to Greatness\" (February 26, 2013)'''\n*During practice, Uriah Hall's hard punching while sparring with Luke Barnatt rubbed him the wrong way, which caused him and the rest of the team to think that Hall was not a team player.\n*Hall later ignited a blow-up at the house when he was asked by Gilbert Smith who he wanted to fight next and he answered Barnatt, his own teammate. This caused Team Jones to confront the Jamaican, asking him to join their team. But Hall's remark towards Adam Cella's girlfriend caused a rift between both teams.\n*The coaches took their teams bowling at [[Red Rock Casino]]. Sonnen then challenged Jones to a bowling match, betting whoever bowls the lowest score after three-frames had to coach a session for the other team while wearing that team's jersey. Jones lost and agreed to the terms.\n*Josh Samman told the coaches about almost losing his left leg to a blood clot in the past.\n*Josh Samman defeated Tor Troeng via KO (punches) in the first round.\n*Now with the red team in control, Jones matched his number one pick Clint Hester against Jimmy Quinlan.\n*Sonnen liked it, thinking it was a bad match style-wise for Hester.\n\n;'''Episode 7: \"His Fist on My Face\" (March 5, 2013)'''\n*After winning his fight, Josh Samman's recurring leg pain landed him in the hospital emergency room.\n*The guys got a night out when their coaches took them to [[Hooters]] to lift their spirits. However, not wanting outside distraction, Uriah Hall alienated himself from the group because he would rather concentrate on fighting.\n*Jimmy Quinlan defeated Clint Hester via submission (rear naked choke) in the second round.\n*After the fight, Sonnen entered the red team's locker room to uphold the bet he and Jones made during bowling three frames. Much to Jones' dislike, Sonnen gave him a black jersey to wear while coaching.\n*The final match-up of the opening round by default was Dylan Andrews vs. Zak Cummings.\n\n;'''Episode 8: \"Bag of Tools\" (March 12, 2013)'''\n*Bubba McDaniel was stressed over being picked for the wildcard match-up and vented to Adam Cella.\n*This season's \"Coaches Challenge\" put Jones and Sonnen against each other in three different \"dig it\" competitions while driving [[excavators]]. Sonnen won the competition earning $10,000 for his team ($1,500 per fighter).\n*Dylan Andrews defeated Zak Cummings via majority decision.\n*After the fight, Dana White forced the coaches to decide on a wildcard match-up.\n*Jones was left to make a difficult decision when he had to pick between his training partner McDaniel and Clint Hester, whom he calls a cool guy. Jones picked McDaniel because of all the years of hard work, while Sonnen picked Kevin Casey.\n\n;'''Episode 9: \"The Wild Card\" (March 19, 2013)'''\n*Bubba McDaniel became emotional while talking about his family.\n*During a training session, the coaches spent time building up McDaniel's confidence.\n*Josh Samman called out Jimmy Quinlan because he wanted to be the next one to do that after McDaniel.\n*Both teams celebrated Thanksgiving by having a huge feast at the house. However, McDaniel and Casey were unable to eat due to having to make weight.\n*Bubba McDanie'''l''' defeated Kevin Casey via TKO (retirement) in the second round.\n*McDaniel celebrated his win while Casey explained that a kidney failure during the fight was the reason that he could not answer the bell.\n*The quarter final matchups were announced as:\n:*Collin Hart vs. Kelvin Gastelum\n:*Dylan Andrews vs. Luke Barnatt\n:*Josh Samman vs. Jimmy Quinlan\n:*Bubba McDaniel vs. Uriah Hall\n\n;'''Episode 10: \"The Quarterfinals Begin\" (March 26, 2013)'''\n*Both teams were surprised with the match up between Bubba McDaniel and Uriah Hall, causing McDaniel to stress out over his tough opponent.\n*Kelvin Gastelum got a surprise visit from Ronda Rousey, who guest coached Team Sonnen in [[judo]] techniques.\n*The first two match-ups faced off with four fighters trying to secure a spot in the final four.\n*Kelvin Gastelum defeated Collin Hart via TKO (punches) in the first round.\n*Former world heavyweight boxing champion [[Mike Tyson]] made a guest appearance and gave both teams motivation as he sat cageside during the quarterfinal matches.\n*Dylan Andrews defeated Luke Barnatt via TKO (punches) in the third round.\n*The latter fight was notable in which Andrews, who was the last overall pick, defeated the first overall pick Barnatt.\n\n;'''Episode 11: \"Vicious\" (April 2, 2013)'''\n*Bubba McDaniel did not participate in team practice due to back pain. This led Jones to tell Clint Hester to stay ready.\n*McDaniel told Jones that he still wanted to fight, but felt frustrated because he believed White was putting him on the most difficult route.\n*McDaniel went to get his blood tested at the doctor due to back pain.\n*Josh Samman defeated Jimmy Quinlan via submission (punches) in the first round.\n*Regarding Samman's double punch submission, Jones stated that this was not a move that he recommended.\n*The doctor visited the gym and told McDaniel that his back pain was most likely a pulled muscle. Feeling motivated with the news, McDaniel got back to practice.\n*Uriah Hall told his team in the locker room that he had heard that McDaniel was scared to fight him. Sonnen then remarked on how that type of attention on Hall was well deserved, calling him the most talented and hard working fighter on his team.\n*During the weigh-ins, Jones was not happy with McDaniel's submissive body language during the stare-off with Hall.\n*Uriah Hall defeated Bubba McDaniel via KO (punch) in the first round.\n*After the fight, McDaniel remained on the canvas due to a pain in his eye. He then admitted to not remembering what he was hit with. White then stated that Hall was the \"nastiest fighter in TUF history.\"\n*The semifinal match-ups were announced as:\n:*Josh Samman vs. Kelvin Gastelum\n:*Dylan Andrews vs. Uriah Hall\n\n;'''Episode 12: \"The Semis\" (April 9, 2013)'''\n*The final four fighters along with both coaches visit the ''[[Harley Davidson Motorcycles]]'' showroom to customize their bikes as one of the prizes for being ''The Ultimate Fighter''.\n*Kelvin Gastelum defeated Josh Samman via submission (rear naked choke) in the first round.\n*Uriah Hall defeated Dylan Andrews via TKO (punches) in the second round.\n*White was amazed at how Hall had managed to get up to the top position from the bottom, having given up a [[Kimura lock|kimura]] attempt to unload strikes to Andrews' head.\n*The black team had two fighters in the finale; that meant Sonnen, as the winning coach, would get a custom Harley Davidson motorcycle.\n*The finalists were announced as Hall and Gastelum, both of Team Sonnen.\n\n==Tournament bracket==\n{{Round16\n\n\n||{{color box|#C41E3A|border=silver}} '''Colin Hart'''|'''UD'''|{{color box|black|border=silver}} Kevin Casey|2\n||{{color box|#C41E3A|border=silver}} Fans chose the winners of the season bonuses.\n\n*Fight of the Season: Dylan Andrews vs. Luke Barnatt\n*Knockout of the Season: Uriah Hall\n*Submission of the Season: Kelvin Gastelum\n\n==The Ultimate Fighter 17 Finale==\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=The Ultimate Fighter: Team Jones vs. Team Sonnen Finale\n|image=TUF Finale Jones Sonnen.jpg\n|promotion=[[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]\n|date=April 13, 2013\n|venue=[[Mandalay Bay Events Center]]\n|city= [[Las Vegas Valley|Las Vegas]], [[Nevada]]\n|attendance=5,918{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/04/the-ultimate-fighter-17-finale-officially-sells-5382-tickets-for-569k-gate|title=The Ultimate Fighter 17 Finale $569K gate|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|date=2012-04-30|access-date=2013-04-30|author=Staff}}\n|gate=$569,075\n|previousevent = [[UFC on Fuel TV: Mousasi vs. Latifi]]\n|followingevent = [[2013 in UFC#UFC on Fox: Henderson vs. Melendez|UFC on Fox: Henderson vs. Melendez]]\n}}\n\n''The Ultimate Fighter: Team Jones vs. Team Sonnen Finale'' (also known as ''The Ultimate Fighter 17 Finale'') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]. It took place on April 13, 2013, at the [[Mandalay Bay Events Center]] in [[Las Vegas Valley|Las Vegas]], [[Nevada]].{{cite web |url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/02/ufc-targets-las-vegas-mandalay-bay-for-the-ultimate-fighter-17-finale-in-april|title=UFC targets Las Vegas' Mandalay Bay for The Ultimate Fighter 17 Finale in April|work=mmajunkie.com |date=February 18, 2013 |access-date=February 18, 2013}}\n\n===Background===\n\nThe event was expected to feature a [[UFC Flyweight Championship|Flyweight Championship]] bout between the then current champion [[Demetrious Johnson (fighter)|Demetrious Johnson]], and challenger [[John Moraga]], as well as the [[Middleweight (MMA)|middleweight]] tournament finals.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/02/demetrious-johnson-defends-ufc-flyweight-title-vs-john-moraga-at-tuf-17-finale|title=Demetrious Johnson defends UFC flyweight title vs. John Moraga at TUF 17 Finale|publisher=MMA Junkie| author=Staff |date=2013-02-11 |access-date=2013-02-11}} However, Johnson was forced out of the bout with an injury and Moraga was removed from the event as well.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/02/demetrious-johnson-injured-title-fight-with-john-moraga-off-tuf-17-finale|title=Demetrious Johnson injured, title fight with John Moraga off TUF 17 Finale|publisher=MMA Junkie| author=Staff |date=2013-02-26 |access-date=2013-02-26}} As a result, the main event featured a bout between the former [[List of WEC champions#Featherweight Championship|WEC Featherweight Champion]] [[Urijah Faber]] and long time [[Bantamweight (MMA)|bantamweight]] contender [[Scott Jorgensen]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/02/urijah-faber-vs-scott-jorgensen-is-the-ultimate-fighter-17-finales-new-headlin|title=Urijah Faber-Scott Jorgensen is The Ultimate Fighter 17 Finale's new headliner|publisher=MMA Junkie| author=Staff |date=2013-02-27 |access-date=2013-02-27}}\n\nThe winner of the [[Miesha Tate]] vs. [[Cat Zingano]] bout was expected to coach against [[Ronda Rousey]] for the next season of [[The Ultimate Fighter]].{{cite web|url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Ronda-Rousey-Winner-of-Miesha-TateCat-Zingano-to-Coach-Coed-TUF-18-50979|title=Ronda Rousey, Winner of Miesha Tate-Cat Zingano to Coach Coed 'TUF 18'|publisher=sherdog.com| author=Mike Whitman |date=2013-03-16 |access-date=2013-03-16}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card |access-date=2013-03-30}}\n\n===Bonus Awards===\nFighters were awarded $50,000 bonuses.{{cite web |url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/04/tuf-17-finale-bonuses-zingano-tate-browne-pineda-earn-50000 |title=TUF 17 Finale bonuses: Zingano, Tate, Browne, Pineda earn $50,000|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|author=Staff |access-date=April 14, 2013}}\n* '''Fight of the Night: Cat Zingano vs. Miesha Tate'''\n* '''Knockout of the Night: Travis Browne'''\n* '''Submission of the Night: Daniel Pineda'''\n\n===Reported payout===\n\nThe following is the reported payout to the fighters as reported to the [[Nevada State Athletic Commission]]. It does not include sponsor money and also does not include the UFC's traditional \"fight night\" bonuses.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/04/tuf-17-finale-salaries-urijah-faber-cole-miller-top-fighter-payroll|title=TUF 17 Finale salaries: Urijah Faber, Cole Miller top fighter payroll|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|date=2013-04-14|access-date=2013-04-14|author=Staff}}\n* Uriah Faber: $110,000 (includes $55,000 win bonus) def. Scott Jorgensen: $23,500\n* Kelvin Gastelum: $16,000 (includes $8,000 win bonus) def. Uriah Hall: $8,000\n* Cat Zingano: $14,000 (includes $7,000 win bonus) def. Miesha Tate: $28,000\n* Travis Browne: $40,000 (includes $20,000 win bonus) def. Gabriel Gonzaga: $24,000\n* Robert McDaniel: $16,000 (includes $8,000 win bonus) def. April 6, 2013\n|venue= [[Ericsson Globe Arena]]\n|city= [[Stockholm]], [[Sweden]]\n|attendance=14,506{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/04/ufc-on-fuel-tv-9-draws-a-reported-14506-attendance-for-2-71-million-live-gate|title=UFC on FUEL TV 9 draws a reported 14,506 attendance for $2.71 million Junkie|date=2013-04-06|access-date=2013-04-06|archive-date=2023-04-04|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20230404105440/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/04/ufc-on-fuel-tv-9-draws-a-reported-14506-attendance-for-2-71-million-live-gate|url-status=dead}}\n|gate=$2,710,530\n|buyrate=\n|previousevent=[[UFC Finale]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC on Fuel TV: Mousasi vs. Latifi''''' (also known as '''''UFC on Fuel TV 9''''' or '''''UFC Sweden 2''''') was a mixed martial arts event held on April 6, 2013, at the [[Ericsson Globe Arena]] in [[Stockholm, Sweden]].{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/ufc-sweden-2-april-6-release|title=UFC returns to Sweden on April 6|publisher=ufc.com|date=2013-01-19|access-date=2013-01-19}} The event was broadcast live on [[Fox Sports 2|Fuel TV]].\n\n==Background==\nThis event was the second event that the UFC has hosted in [[Sweden]], following [[UFC on Fuel TV: Gustafsson vs. Silva]] which took place in 2012, and was the fastest selling European event in UFC history, selling out in just three hours, partly due to alleged involvement from [[Ticket resale|ticket re-sellers]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/26978/ufc-on-fuel-tv-2-in-sweden-sells-out-in-three-hours.mma|title=UFC on FUEL TV 2 in Sweden sells out in three hours|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2012-01-14|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20120116215455/http://mmajunkie.com/news/26978/ufc-on-fuel-tv-2-in-sweden-sells-out-in-three-hours.mma|archive-date=2012-01-16}}{{cite web |url=http://www.mmafightbetting.com/ufc-sweden-swedish-mma-fans-discouraged-by-inflated-ticket-prices/ |title=Archived copy |access-date=2013-03-08 |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130308231523/http://www.mmafightbetting.com/ufc-sweden-swedish-mma-fans-discouraged-by-inflated-ticket-prices/ |archive-date=2013-03-08 }}\n\nThe event was expected to be headlined by top light heavyweight contender [[Alexander Gustafsson]] and former [[List of Dream champions|Dream Middleweight and Light Heavyweight Champion]] and former [[List of Strikeforce champions|Strikeforce Light Heavyweight Champion]], and promotional newcomer [[Gegard Mousasi]].{{cite web |url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2013/1/19/3893586/alexander-gustafsson-vs-gegard-mousasi-slated-for-april-in-sweden |title=Alexander Gustafsson vs. Gegard Mousasi slated for April in Sweden |publisher=mmafighting.com |author=Ariel Helwani|date=2013-01-19|access-date=2013-01-19}}{{cite web |url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/8859636/alexander-gustafsson-face-former-strikeforce-titleholder-gegard-mousasi |title=Alexander Gustafsson set to fight |publisher=espn.go.com.com |author=Franklin McNeil|date=2013-01-19|access-date=2013-01-19}} However, on April 2 it was announced by the Swedish MMA Federation, that Gustafsson would not be cleared to participate at the event due to a cut he received during a sparring session on March 30.{{cite web |url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/04/swedish-mma-federation-alexander-gustafsson-not-cleared-to-fight-at-ufc-on-fuel |title=Swedish MMA Federation: Alexander Gustafsson not cleared for UFC on FUEL TV 9 |publisher=mmajunkie.com |author=Staff |date=2013-04-02 |access-date=2013-04-02 |archive-date=2013-04-04 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130404094758/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/04/swedish-mma-federation-alexander-gustafsson-not-cleared-to-fight-at-ufc-on-fuel |url-status=dead }} Gustafsson was replaced by UFC newcomer [[Ilir Latifi]], one of his main training partners.{{cite web |url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/04/gustafsson-teammate-ilir-latifi-meets-mousasi-in-ufc-on-fuel-tv-9-main-event |title=Gustafsson teammate Ilir Latifi meets Mousasi in UFC on FUEL TV 9 main event |publisher=mmajunkie.com |author=Staff |date=2013-04-02 |access-date=2013-04-02 |archive-date=2013-04-04 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130404094744/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/04/gustafsson-teammate-ilir-latifi-meets-mousasi-in-ufc-on-fuel-tv-9-main-event |url-status=dead }} The main event was also contested at three rounds, instead of the organization's customary five.\n\nA bout between [[Robbie Peralta]] and [[Akira Corassani]], previously linked to [[UFC 156]],{{cite web |url=http://www.mmamania.com/2013/1/4/3837472/robbie-peralta-vs-akira-corassani-featherweight-bout-added-to|title=Robbie Peralta vs. Akira Corassani featherweight bout added to February's UFC 156|publisher=mmamania.com|last=Bohn|first=Mike|date=2013-01-04|access-date=2013-01-04}} was moved to this event after an illness sidelined Corassani for a short period of time.{{cite web |url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/01/akira-corassani-vs-robbie-peralta-targeted-for-ufc-on-fuel-tv-9-in-sweden |title=Akira Corassani vs. Robbie Peralta targeted for UFC on FUEL TV 9 in Sweden |publisher=MMAJunkie.com |author=John Morgan and Steven Marrocco |date=2013-01-20 |access-date=2013-01-20 |archive-date=2013-01-20 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130120181143/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/01/akira-corassani-vs-robbie-peralta-targeted-for-ufc-on-fuel-tv-9-in-sweden |url-status=dead }}\n\nThis card featured the UFC debut of [[Conor McGregor]].\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card (Fuel TV)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Light Heavyweight\n|[[Gegard Mousasi]]\n|def.\n|[[Ilir 2013, at the [[MTS Centre]] in [[Winnipeg, Manitoba]], [[Canada]].{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/ufc-161-winnipeg-press-release|title=UFC hits Winnipeg for UFC 161 on June 15|publisher=ufc.com|author=Staff |date=2013-03-05|access-date=2013-03-05}}\n\n==Background==\nWhile the UFC has hosted several events in the past in Canada, the event was the first that the organization has hosted in [[Manitoba]].{{cite web|url=https://nationalpost.com/sports/its-official-winnipeg-will-host-ufc-161|title=It's official: Winnipeg will host UFC 161 |publisher=sports.nationalpost.com|author=Canadian Press |date=2013-03-05|access-date=2013-03-05}}{{cite web|url=https://www.tsn.ca/story/?id=417396|title=UFC comes to Winnipeg for first time with show set for June |publisher=tsn.ca.com|author=Canadian Press |date=2013-03-05|access-date=2013-03-05}}\n\nThe event was expected to feature an Interim [[UFC Bantamweight Championship|Bantamweight Championship]] bout between the current champion [[Renan Barão]], and former [[List of WEC champions#Bantamweight Championship|WEC Bantamweight Champion]] and top contender [[Eddie Wineland]].{{cite web|url=https://www.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2013/03/06/renan-barao-eddie-wineland-ufc-162-headliner/1969311/|title=Renan Barao vs. Eddie Wineland to headline UFC 161|publisher=USAToday.com|author=Matt Erickson|date=2013-03-06|access-date=2013-03-06}}{{cite web|url=http://www.mmamania.com/2013/3/6/4071642/renan-barao-vs-eddie-wineland-ufc-161-winnipeg|title=Renan Barao vs. Eddie Wineland title fight set to headline UFC 161 in Winnipeg|publisher=MMAmania.com|author=Jesse Holland |date=2013-03-06|access-date=2013-03-06}} However, on May 21 it was confirmed that Barão had pulled out of the bout, citing a foot injury.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/05/injured-champ-renan-barao-officially-out-of-ufc-161-headliner|title=Injured champ Renan Barao officially out of UFC 161 headliner|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=2013-05-21|access-date=2013-05-21|archive-date=2013-06-07|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130607120407/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/05/injured-champ-renan-barao-officially-out-of-ufc-161-headliner|url-status=dead}} With, Barão's departure, Wineland was removed from the card as well.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmamania.com/2013/5/22/4356372/eddie-wineland-crushed-renan-barao-withdrawal-ufc-161-rescheduled-mma|title=Eddie Wineland 'crushed' by Renan Barao's withdrawal from UFC 161; hopes fight is rescheduled|publisher=MMAmania.com|author=Mike Bohn |date=2013-05-22|access-date=2013-05-22}} As a result, the scheduled bout between [[Rashad Evans]] and [[Dan Henderson]] was moved up the card and served as the headliner in a three round bout.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/05/with-renan-barao-hurt-rashad-evans-vs-dan-henderson-headlines-ufc-161|title=With Renan Barao hurt, Rashad Evans vs. Dan Henderson headlines UFC 161|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-05-21|access-date=2013-05-21|archive-date=2013-06-07|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130607121112/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/05/with-renan-barao-hurt-rashad-evans-vs-dan-henderson-headlines-ufc-161|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[TJ Waldburger]] was expected to face [[Sean Pierson]] at the event. However, Waldburger was pulled from the event and replaced by [[Kenny Robertson]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmaweekly.com/tj-waldburger-out-ufc-161-match-up-now-pits-sean-pierson-vs-kenny-robertson|title=TJ Waldburger Out, UFC 161 Match-up Now Pits Sean Pierson vs. Kenny Robertson |publisher=MMAweekly.com|author=Staff |date=2013-05-02|access-date=2013-05-02}}\n\n[[Stipe Miocic]] was scheduled to face returning veteran [[Soa Palelei]] on the undercard, but due to the change in the main event, UFC bolstered the main card and replaced Palelei with [[Roy Nelson (fighter)|Roy Nelson]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/05/usa-today-nelson-vs-miocic-added-to-ufc-161-six-fight-main-card-possible|title=USA TODAY: Nelson vs. Miocic added to UFC 161, six-fight main card possible|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=2013-05-21|access-date=2013-05-21|archive-date=2013-06-07|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130607120946/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/05/usa-today-nelson-vs-miocic-added-to-ufc-161-six-fight-main-card-possible|url-status=dead}} As a result, Palelei was removed from the card, but attended fight week activities as potential replacement for either two heavyweight fights at the event.{{cite web|url=http://www.fightnewsaustralia.com/index.php/225-soa-palelei-off-ufc-161-card-stays-as-backup|title=Soa Palelei off UFC 161 card; remains as a backup|publisher=fightnewsaustralia.com|author=Staff|date=2013-05-22|access-date=2013-05-22|archive-date=2013-08-30|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130830123922/http://fightnewsaustralia.com/index.php/225-soa-palelei-off-ufc-161-card-stays-as-backup|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Isaac Vallie-Flagg]] was expected to face [[Sam Stout]] at the event. However, Vallie-Flagg was forced out with a back injury and was replaced by [[James Krause (fighter)|James Krause]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/05/james-krause-replaces-isaac-vallie-flagg-meets-sam-stout-at-ufc-161|title=James Krause replaces Isaac Vallie-Flagg, meets Sam Stout at UFC 161|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-05-29|access-date=2013-05-29|archive-date=2013-06-08|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130608220925/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/05/james-krause-replaces-isaac-vallie-flagg-meets-sam-stout-at-ufc-161|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Antônio Rogério Nogueira]] was expected to face [[Maurício Rua]] in a rematch in the co-main event. However, Nogueira pulled out of the bout in the days leading up to the event citing a back injury.{{cite web|url=http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2013/6/2/4389460/report-antonio-rogerio-nogueira-out-of-ufc-161-due-to-back-injury|title=Antonio Rogerio Nogueria out at UFC 161 due to back [[Chael Sonnen]] was briefly linked as a replacement for Nogueira.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/06/chael-sonnen-reportedly-replaces-nogueira-meets-shogun-rua-at-ufc-161|title=Chael However, an alleged visa issue created a problem for Sonnen to get into Canada, resulting in Rua being pulled from the event altogether.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/06/visa-issues-reportedly-scrap-sonnen-rua-at-ufc-161-jimmo-pokrajac-head-to-main|title=Visa issues reportedly scrap Sonnen-Rua at UFC 161, Jimmo-Pokrajac on Fox: Johnson vs. Moraga]]\n}}\n'''''UFC 162: Silva vs. Weidman''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event on July 6, 2013, at the [[MGM Grand Garden Arena]] in [[Las Vegas, Nevada]].{{cite web | url= http://www.ufc.com/event/UFC162 | title= UFC 162: Fight Card | publisher= Ultimate Fighting Championship (UFC) | access-date= June 29, 2013}}\n\n==Background==\nThe main event was a [[UFC Middleweight Championship]] bout between long time title holder [[Anderson Silva]] and undefeated top contender [[Chris Weidman]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmamania.com/2013/3/6/4071678/anderson-silva-vs-chris-weidman-official-for-ufc-162-headliner-on|title=Anderson Silva vs. Chris Weidman official for UFC 162 headliner on July 6 in Las Vegas|publisher=MMAmania.com|author=Brian Hemminger |date=2013-03-06|access-date=2013-03-06}}\n\nAlso, co-featured on the card was expected to be a bout between top featherweight contenders [[Ricardo Lamas]] and [[Chan Sung Jung]]; however on June 14, it was announced that Jung had been pulled from the Lamas bout and would replace an injured [[Anthony Pettis]] to face [[José Aldo]] on August 3, 2013, at [[UFC 163]].{{cite web|url=http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2013/6/14/4429832/ufc-163-pettis-out-chan-sung-jung-vs-jose-aldo|title=Anthony Pettis out of UFC 163 with injury, Chan Sung Jung to face Jose Aldo|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Nate Wilcox|date=2013-06-14|access-date=2013-06-14|archive-date=2013-10-16|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20131016062754/http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2013/6/14/4429832/ufc-163-pettis-out-chan-sung-jung-vs-jose-aldo|url-status=dead}} As a result, Lamas was pulled from the event.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/06/ricardo-lamas-off-ufc-162-tim-boetsch-mark-munoz-promoted-to-main-card|title=Ricardo Lamas off UFC 162, Tim Boetsch-Mark Munoz promoted to main card|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Matt Erickson|date=2013-06-18|access-date=2013-06-18|archive-date=2013-06-21|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130621060613/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/06/ricardo-lamas-off-ufc-162-tim-boetsch-mark-munoz-promoted-to-main-card|url-status=dead}}\n\nA bout between [[Thiago Silva (fighter)|Thiago Silva]] and [[Rafael Cavalcante]], briefly linked to this event, was moved to [[UFC on Fuel TV: Nogueira vs. Werdum]].{{cite web|url=http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2013/3/10/4086098/report-thiago-silva-vs-rafael-cavalcante-ufc-on-fuel-tv-10-mma-news|title=Thiago Silva vs. Rafael Cavalcante moved to UFC on Fuel TV 10|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Mookie Alexander |date=2013-03-10|access-date=2013-03-10}}\n\n[[John Makdessi]] was expected to face [[Edson Barboza]] at the event; however, Makdessi, pulled out of the bout citing an injury and was replaced by [[Rafaello Oliveira]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2013/5/30/4382024/edson-barboza-faces-rafaello-oliveira-at-ufc-162-following-john|title=Edson Barboza faces Rafaello Oliveira at UFC 162 following John Makdessi martial arts event held on May 18, 2013, at the [[Arena Jaraguá]] in [[Jaraguá do Sul]], [[Brazil]].{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/ufc-on-fx-8-fight-card-updates|title=UFC Returns to Brazil for Rockhold vs. Belfort|publisher=ufc.com|date=2013-03-06|access-date=2013-03-06|author=Staff}}{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/02/ufc-on-fx-8-belfort-vs-rockhold-set-for-may-18-in-jaragua-do-sul-brazil|title='UFC on FX 8: Belfort vs. Rockhold' set for May 18 in Jaragua do Sul, Brazil|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|date=2013-02-14|access-date=2013-02-14|author=Staff|archive-date=2023-05-31|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20230531213127/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/02/ufc-on-fx-8-belfort-vs-rockhold-set-for-may-18-in-jaragua-do-sul-brazil|url-status=dead}} The event was broadcast live on [[FUEL TV]] and [[FX (TV channel)|FX]].\n\n==Background==\nThe event was headlined by a middleweight bout between perennial multi-divisional contender [[Vitor Belfort]] and the final [[List of Strikeforce champions|Strikeforce Middleweight Champion]] and promotional newcomer and future UFC Middleweight Champion [[Luke Rockhold]].\n\n[[Manvel Gamburyan]] was expected to face [[Hacran Dias]] at the event. However, Gamburyan was forced to pull out of the bout citing an injury and was replaced by [[Nik Lentz]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/03/nik-lentz-replaces-manny-gamburyan-meets-hacran-dias-at-ufc-on-fx-8|title=Nik Lentz replaces Manny Gamburyan, meets Hacran Dias at UFC on FX 8|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-03-25|access-date=2013-03-25|archive-date=2013-03-27|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130327232346/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/03/nik-lentz-replaces-manny-gamburyan-meets-hacran-dias-at-ufc-on-fx-8|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Marcos Vinicius (fighter)|Marcos Vinícius]] was expected to face [[Iuri Alcântara]] at the event. However, Vinicius was forced out of the bout citing an injury and was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Iliarde Santos]]{{cite web |url=http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2013/4/1/4169008/ufc-fx-8-yuri-alcantara--vs-marcos-vinicius-injury |title=Vinicius out with knee injury, Alcantara awaits new opponent |publisher=bloodyelbow.com |author=Zane Simon |date=2013-04-01 |access-date=2013-04-01 |archive-date=2016-03-04 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20160304052638/http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2013/4/1/4169008/ufc-fx-8-yuri-alcantara--vs-marcos-vinicius-injury |url-status=dead }}{{cite web|url=http://www.tatame.com.br/iliarde-assina-com-o-ufc-e-encara-marajo-no-evento-de-jaragua-do-sul/#|title=Iliarde assina com o UFC e encara Marajó no evento de Jaraguá do Sul (Brazilian Portuguese)|publisher=tatame.com.br|last=Cruz|first=Guilherme|date=2013-04-02|access-date=2013-04-02}}{{Dead link|date=July 2018 |bot=InternetArchiveBot |fix-attempted=no }}\n\n[[Cezar Ferreira]] was originally scheduled to face [[CB Dollaway]]. However, on April 1, it was revealed that Dollaway had to withdraw from the bout due to injury and he was replaced by [[Chris Camozzi]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/04/chris-camozzi-in-for-injured-c-b-dollaway-vs-cezar-ferreira-at-ufc-on-fx-8|title=Chris Camozzi in for injured C.B. Dollaway vs. Cezar Ferreira at UFC on FX 8|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2013-04-01|access-date=2013-04-01|archive-date=2013-04-03|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130403234320/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/04/chris-camozzi-in-for-injured-c-b-dollaway-vs-cezar-ferreira-at-ufc-on-fx-8|url-status=dead}} Then on April 7, it was confirmed that Ferreira had pulled out of the fight as well, citing an injury and was replaced by [[Rafael Natal]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/04/chris-camozzi-vs-rafael-natal-slated-for-ufc-on-fx-8-in-brazil|title=Chris Camozzi vs. Rafael Natal slated for UFC on FX 8 in Brazil|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2013-04-07|access-date=2013-04-07|archive-date=2013-04-09|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130409081332/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/04/chris-camozzi-vs-rafael-natal-slated-for-ufc-on-fx-8-in-brazil|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Costas Philippou]] had to withdraw from his fight against [[Ronaldo Souza]], after suffering a cut above his eye, and was replaced by [[Chris Camozzi]]. Camozzi's scheduled opponent [[Rafael Natal]] instead faced promotional newcomer João Zeferino.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2013/5/3/4296354/injury-forces-costa-philippou-out-of-jacare-souza-fight-at-ufc-on-fx-8|title=Injury forces Costa Philippou out of 'Jacare' Souza fight at UFC on FX 8 |publisher=mmafighting.com|date=May 3, 2013|access-date=May 3, 2013|author=Ariel Helwani}}{{cite web|url=http://www.mmamania.com/2013/5/3/4296956/chris-camozzi-jacare-souza-ufc-on-fx-8-joao-zeferinoto-brazil-mma|title=Chris Camozzi gets 'Jacare' Souza at UFC on FX 8, Joao Zeferino debuts opposite Rafael Natal |publisher=mmamania.com|date=May 3, 2013|access-date=May 3, 2013|author=Jesse Holland}}\n\n[[Paulo Thiago]] was originally expected to face [[Lance Benoist]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/03/paulo-thiago-vs-lance-benoist-added-to-ufc-on-fx-8-in-brazil|title=Paulo Thiago vs. Lance Benoist added to UFC on FX 8 in Brazil|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|date=March 1, 2013|access-date=March 1, 2013|author=Staff|archive-date=March 4, 2013|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130304080026/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/03/paulo-thiago-vs-lance-benoist-added-to-ufc-on-fx-8-in-brazil|url-status=dead}} However, on April 20, it was announced that Benoist had to pull out of the bout due to an injury and was replaced by UFC newcomer [[Michel Prazeres]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/04/report-benoist-hurt-tiago-dos-santos-meets-paulo-thiago-at-ufc-on-fx-8|title=Benoist out, Michel Prazeres meets Paulo Thiago at UFC on FX Night: Shogun vs. Sonnen]]\n}}\n'''''UFC 163: Aldo vs. Korean Zombie''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held on August 3, 2013, at the [[HSBC Arena (Rio de Janeiro)|HSBC Arena]] in [[Rio de Janeiro, Brazil]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/03/report-ufc-163-aldo-vs-pettis-booked-for-rio-de-janeiros-hsbc-arena|title=Report: 'UFC 163: Aldo vs. Pettis' booked for Rio de Janeiro's HSBC Arena|publisher=MMAJunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-03-12|access-date=2013-03-12|archive-date=2013-03-14|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130314101533/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/03/report-ufc-163-aldo-vs-pettis-booked-for-rio-de-janeiros-hsbc-arena|url-status=dead}}\n\n==Background==\nThe main event was expected to feature current [[UFC Featherweight Championship|UFC Featherweight Champion]], [[José Aldo]] taking on future [[UFC Lightweight Championship|UFC Lightweight Champion]], [[Anthony Pettis]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/02/anthony-pettis-drops-to-145-meets-ufc-featherweight-champ-jose-aldo-on-aug-3|title=Anthony Pettis drops to 145, meets UFC champ Jose Aldo on Aug. 3|publisher=MMAJunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=2013-02-05|access-date=2013-02-05|archive-date=2013-02-07|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130207135757/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/02/anthony-pettis-drops-to-145-meets-ufc-featherweight-champ-jose-aldo-on-aug-3|url-status=dead}} However, in mid-June Pettis pulled out of the bout citing a knee injury and was replaced by [[Chan Sung Jung]].{{cite web|url=http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2013/6/14/4429832/ufc-163-pettis-out-chan-sung-jung-vs-jose-aldo|title=Anthony Pettis out of UFC 163 with injury, Chan Sung Jung to face Jose Aldo|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Nate Wilcox|date=2013-06-14|access-date=2013-06-14|archive-date=2013-10-16|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20131016062754/http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2013/6/14/4429832/ufc-163-pettis-out-chan-sung-jung-vs-jose-aldo|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Josh Koscheck]] was expected to face [[Demian Maia]] at the event. However, Koscheck was forced out of the bout with an injury and as a result, Maia was pulled from the card as well.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/07/josh-koscheck-injured-out-of-ufc-163-fight-with-demian-maia-in-rio|title=Josh Koscheck injured, out of UFC 163 fight with Demian Maia in Rio|publisher=MMAJunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-07-12|access-date=2013-07-12|archive-date=2013-07-13|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130713181036/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/07/josh-koscheck-injured-out-of-ufc-163-fight-with-demian-maia-in-rio|url-status=dead}}\n\nPromotional newcomer [[Robert Drysdale]] was expected to face Ednaldo Oliveira at the event. However, Drysdale pulled out of the bout in mid-July citing a lingering staph infection.{{cite web|url=http://www.reviewjournal.com/sports/mma-ufc/las-vegan-robert-drysdale-forced-pull-out-ufc-debut|title=Las Vegan Robert Drysdale forced to pull out of UFC debut |publisher=reviewjournal.com|author=Adam Hill|date=2013-07-16|access-date=2013-07-16}} Oliveira faced UFC newcomer [[Francimar Barroso]] at the event.{{cite web|url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Report-Robert-Drysdale-off-UFC-163-with-Staph-Francimar-Bodao-Tapped-as-Replacement-53957|title=Report: Robert Drysdale off UFC 163 with Staph; Francimar 'Bodao' Tapped as Replacement|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Mike Whitman|date=2013-07-17|access-date=2013-07-17}}\n\n[[Phil Harris (fighter)|Phil Harris]] was expected to face [[John Lineker]] at the event. However, Harris was forced out of the bout and Lineker faced promotional newcomer José Maria Tomé.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/07/jose-maria-tome-replaces-phil-harris-meets-john-lineker-at-ufc-163|title=Jose Maria Tome replaces Phil Harris, meets John Lineker at UFC 163|publisher=MMAJunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-07-18|access-date=2013-07-18|archive-date=2013-07-21|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130721062235/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/07/jose-maria-tome-replaces-phil-harris-meets-john-lineker-at-ufc-163|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Clint Hester]] was expected to face [[Cezar Ferreira]] at the event. However, Hester was forced out of the bout with an injury and was replaced by [[Thiago Santos (fighter)|Thiago Santos]].{{cite web|url=http://mmanytt.se/ufc/clint-hester-skadad-thiago-marreta-moter-nu-cezar-mutante-pa-ufc-163/|title=Clint Hester skadad, Thiago 'Marreta' möter nu Cezar 'Mutante' på UFC 163 |publisher=mmanytt.se|author=Christoferr Esping|date=2013-07-18|access-date=2013-07-18}}\n\n[[Brian Stann]] co-commentated the card with Mike Goldberg due to a prior commitment for Joe Rogan.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/07/brian-stann-replaces-joe-rogan-for-ufc-163-color-commentator-duties-in-brazil|title=Brian Stann replaces Joe Rogan for UFC 163 color commentator duties in Brazil|publisher=MMAJunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-07-24|access-date=2013-07-25|archive-date=2013-07-26|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130726123445/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/07/brian-stann-replaces-joe-rogan-for-ufc-163-color-commentator-duties-in-brazil|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[John Lineker]] missed the 126 pound weight limit for a flyweight non-title fight, weighing in at 129 pounds. As a result, he surrendered 20 percent of his purse to his opponent, José Maria Tomé, and the bout took place as a 129-pound catchweight fight.{{cite
