2]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC Fight Night: Teixeira vs. Bader]]\n}}\n'''''UFC 164: Henderson vs. Pettis 2''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held on August 31, 2013, at the [[BMO Harris Bradley Center]] in [[Milwaukee, Wisconsin]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmamania.com/2013/3/15/4108690/ufc-164-official-aug-31-bradley-center-milwaukee-mma|title=UFC 164 official for Aug. 31 at Bradley Center in Milwaukee|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Jesse Holland|date=2013-03-15|access-date=2013-03-15}}{{cite web|url=http://www.jsonline.com/sports/etc/ufc-plans-return-to-milwaukee-s09627f-198570571.html|title=UFC plans return to Milwaukee|publisher=JSonline.com|author=Staff|date=2013-03-16|access-date=2013-03-16}}\n\n==Background==\nThe main event was expected to be a [[UFC Lightweight Championship]] bout between the current champion [[Benson Henderson]] and top contender [[T. J. Grant]].{{cite web|url=http://msn.foxsports.com/ufc/story/benson-henderson-tj-grant-to-headline-ufc-164-060413|title=Henderson, Grant to headline UFC 164|publisher=msn.foxsports.com|author=A.J. Perez|date=2013-06-04|access-date=2013-06-04}}{{Dead link|date=July 2018 |bot=InternetArchiveBot |fix-attempted=no }} However, Grant was forced out of the bout with an injury and was replaced by [[Anthony Pettis]].{{cite web |url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/07/t-j-grant-injured-anthony-pettis-now-meets-champ-benson-henderson-at-ufc-164 |title=T.J. Grant injured, Anthony Pettis now meets champ Benson Henderson at UFC 164 |publisher=mmajunkie.com |author=John Morgan |date=2013-07-12 |access-date=2013-07-12 |archive-date=2013-07-14 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130714044047/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/07/t-j-grant-injured-anthony-pettis-now-meets-champ-benson-henderson-at-ufc-164 |url-status=dead }} Henderson and Pettis met in 2010 at the final event for [[World Extreme Cagefighting]], [[WEC 53]]. Pettis defeated Henderson via unanimous decision to win the final [[List of WEC champions#Lightweight Championship|WEC Lightweight]] title in a bout that was widely praised by MMA fans and pundits alike as the fight of the year.{{cite web |url=http://www.mmamania.com/2010/12/16/1881318/wec-53-results-anthony-pettis-decisions-ben-henderson-to-win-wec |title=WEC 53 results: Anthony Pettis decisions Ben Henderson to win WEC lightweight title |publisher=mmamania.com |author=Gene Mrosko|date=2010-12-16|access-date=2010-12-16}}\n\nCo-featured was a bout between two former [[UFC Heavyweight Championship|UFC Heavyweight Champions]], [[Frank Mir]] and [[Josh Barnett]].{{cite web |url=http://www.mmamania.com/2013/5/29/4378316/frank-mir-vs-josh-barnett-fight-ufc-164-main-event-milwaukee-mma |title=Frank Mir vs Josh Barnett fight set for UFC 164 in Milwaukee |publisher=mmamania.com |author=Thomas Myers|date=2013-05-29|access-date=2013-05-29}}\n\n[[Ryan Couture]] was expected to face [[Quinn Mulhern]] at this event. However, Mulhern was forced out with a hand injury and was replaced by [[Al Iaquinta]].{{cite web |url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/07/quinn-mulhern-out-al-iaquinta-meets-ryan-couture-at-ufc-164-in-milwaukee |title=Quinn Mulhern out, Al Iaquinta meets Ryan Couture at UFC 164 in Milwaukee |publisher=mmajunkie.com |author=MMAjunkie.com Staff |date=2013-07-16 |access-date=2013-07-16 |archive-date=2013-07-18 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130718150547/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/07/quinn-mulhern-out-al-iaquinta-meets-ryan-couture-at-ufc-164-in-milwaukee |url-status=dead }}\n\n[[Yoel Romero]] was expected to face [[Derek Brunson]] at this event. However, Brunson was forced out with an injury and was replaced by newcomer Brian Houston.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/08/brian-houston-replaces-derek-brunson-meets-yoel-romero-at-ufc-164|title=Brian Houston replaces Derek Brunson, meets Yoel Romero at UFC 164|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=MMAjunkie.com Houston ended up also getting injured and Romero was removed from the card.{{cite web Jared Hamman: $14,000\n\n==See also==\n*[[List of UFC events]]\n*[[2013 in UFC]]\n\n==References==\n{{Reflist}}\n\n{{UFC Events}}\n\n[[Category:Ultimate Fighting Championship flagship events]]\n[[Category:2013 in mixed martial arts]]\n[[Category:Mixed martial arts in Wisconsin]]\n[[Category:Sports in Milwaukee]]\n[[Category:Sports competitions in Milwaukee]]\n[[Category:2013 in sports in Wisconsin]]\n[[Category:Organized events in Milwaukee]]"}}}],"langlinks":[{"lang":"ja","title":"UFC 164"}]},{"pageid":38849614,"ns":0,"title":"The Ultimate Fighter: Brazil 2","revisions":[{"slots":{"main":{"contentmodel":"wikitext","contentformat":"text/x-wiki","content":"{{short description|UFC mixed martial arts TV series and event in 2013}}\n{{Use mdy dates|date=April 2015}}\n{{Infobox television season\n| image =\n| starring = Dana White, [[Antônio Rodrigo Nogueira]], and [[Fabrício Werdum]]\n| network = [[Rede Globo|Globo]], [[Fox Sports 2|Fuel TV]]\n| first_aired = {{Start date|2013|03|17}}\n| last_aired = {{End date|2013|06|02}} \n}}\n\t\n'''''The Ultimate Fighter: Brazil 2''''' is an installment of the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] (UFC)-produced [[reality television]] series ''[[The Ultimate Fighter]]''.\n\nThis season consisted of 12 episodes and a live finale in Brazil. The season tournament finale, along with the coaches fight, took place on June 8, 2013.{{cite web |url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/03/antonio-rodrigo-nogueira-vs-fabricio-werdum-official-for-ufc-on-fuel-tv-10 |title=Antonio Rodrigo Nogueira vs. Fabrício Werdum official for UFC on FUEL TV 10 |publisher=MMAJunkie.com |date=March 12, 2013 |access-date=March 18, 2013}} The show started filming in November 2012 and was in Portuguese. The season was again produced by Floresta, a Brazilian production company, and aired in Brazil on [[Rede Globo|Globo]]. Each episode was offered over the Internet with English subtitles at TUF.tv.{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/27916/tuf-brazil-premiere-streams-sunday-at-1130-p-m-et-elimination-round-matchups-set.mma |title='TUF: Brazil' premiere streams Sunday at 11:30 p.m. ET, elimination-round matchups set |publisher=MMAJunkie.com |date=March 21, 2012 |access-date=March 22, 2012}}\n\nThe UFC held open tryouts for the show in October 2011. The casting call was for [[Welterweight (MMA)|Welterweight]] fighters who are at least 21 years old and have a minimum of two wins in three professional fights. More than 300 contestants showed up, hoping to be one of the 28 fighters selected.{{cite web |url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2012/10/the-ultimate-fighter-brazil-2-tryouts-attract-more-than-500-hopefuls |title='The Ultimate Fighter: Brazil 2' tryouts attract more than 300 UFC hopefuls |date=October 16, 2012 |publisher=MMAJunkie.com |access-date=March 18, 2013}} On October 30, 2012, it was announced that the coaches for the season would be [[Antônio Rodrigo Nogueira]] and [[Fabrício Werdum]].{{cite web |url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2012/10/antonio-rodrigo-nogueira-fabricio-werdum-official-as-coaches-for-tuf-brazil-2 |title=Antonio Rodrigo Nogueira, Fabrício Werdum official as coaches for 'TUF: Brazil 2' |date=October 30, 2012 |publisher=MMAJunkie.com |access-date=March 18, 2013}}\n\n==Cast==\n\n===Coaches===\n{{Col-begin}}\n{{Col-break}}\n;{{color Aldo]]\n*[[Demian Maia]]\n*[[Erick Silva]]\n*[[Antônio Silva (fighter)|Antônio Silva]]\n\n==Episodes==\n; '''Episode 1'''\n* Coaches [[Antônio Rodrigo Nogueira]] and [[Fabrício Werdum]] are introduced to the fighters before the preliminary fights.\n* Fighters are told this season will feature a wildcard match, allowing for the two best losing fighters, as selected by their coaches, to return.\n* The fights to get into the house were divided into two parts, half on this episode and half on the second episode.\n* The preliminary fights began:\n:'''Pedro Iriê''' defeated Wande Lopes Santana by TKO (punches) in the first round.\n:'''Marcio Santos''' defeated Daniel Oliveira by submission (rear naked choke) in the first round.\n:'''William Macário''' defeated Roberto Barros Martins Amorim by TKO (cut) in the first round.\n:'''Thiago de Lima Santos''' defeated Gil Freitas by majority decision after two rounds.\n:'''Neilson Gomes''' defeated Felipe Olivieri by unanimous decision after two rounds.\n:'''Luiz Dutra Jr.''' defeated Robson Ferreira by unanimous decision after two rounds.\n:'''Tiago Alves''' defeated Weguimar de Lucena Xavier by submission (kimura) in the second round.\n\n; '''Episode 2'''\n* The preliminary fights continued:\n:'''Yan Cabral''' defeated Ronaldo Oliveira Silva by submission (rear naked choke) in the first round.\n:'''Cleiton Duarte''' defeated Bruno Dias by split decision after two rounds.\n:'''Leonardo Santos''' defeated Luciano Contini by TKO (injury) in the first round.\n:'''David Vieira''' defeated [[Leandro Silva (mixed martial artist)|Leandro Silva]] by unanimous decision after two rounds.\n:'''Juliano Wandalen''' defeated Henrique Batista by submission (rear naked choke) in the third round.\n:'''Viscardi Andrade''' defeated Thiago Goncalves by majority decision after two rounds.\n:'''Santiago Ponzinibbio''' defeated Thiago Silva by TKO (punches) in the second round.\n* Following the preliminary fights, the two coaches were allowed to choose their teams and a coin was flipped (green for Nogueira, yellow for Werdum). Werdum won and opted to choose the first fight, so Nogueira got to choose the first fighter. The fighters were chosen in the following order:\n\n{| class=\"wikitable\"\n|-\n!Coach\n!1st Pick\n!2nd Pick\n!3rd Pick\n!4th Pick\n!5th Pick\n!6th Pick\n!7th Pick\n|-\n!Nogueira\n|Luiz Dutra Jr.\n|Santiago Ponzinibbio\n|Neilson Gomes\n|Leonardo Santos\n|David Vieira\n|William Macário\n|Cleiton Duarte\n|-\n!Werdum\n|Pedro Iriê\n|Yan Cabral\n|Tiago Alves\n|Viscardi Andrade\n|Marcio Santos\n|Juliano Wandalen\n|Thiago de Lima Santos\n|}\n* While the green team is out training, Team Werdum destroys the mattresses (including their own) as a prank by skipping on them on the pool and sledding them on the hills. This meant that both teams are forced to sleep on the floor for the rest of the season.\n\n; '''Episode 3'''\n* Angered at the mattress destruction, Team Nogueira decided to attempt revenge on the yellow team by spraying shaving foam into their shoes. However, they mistook the coaches' shoes for the fighters' own. In retaliation, Team Werdum flooded the green team's mats and belongings with foam and flipped over their lockers.\n:'''Tiago Alves''' defeated Cleiton Duarte by unanimous decision after two rounds.\n:'''Yan Cabral''' defeated David Vieira by submission (rear naked choke) in the second round.\n\n; '''Episode 4'''\n* Yan Cabral of Team Werdum is eliminated because of a broken hand and is replaced by Daniel Oliveira, whom Marcio Santos defeated in the elimination round.\n* Neilson Gomes of Team Nogueira is also eliminated due to a torn ligament in his knee and is replaced by Thiago Goncalves, whom Andrade defeated in the elimination round. They will have a rematch to see who will be in the quarterfinals.\n* Former heavyweight champion [[Junior dos Santos|Junior \"Cigano\" dos Santos]] visited Team Nogueira and gave advice to Goncalves.\n:'''Viscardi Andrade''' defeated Thiago Goncalves by TKO (punches) in the first round.\n* After the win, Andrade taunted Nogueira and created tension between the two teams.\n\n; '''Episode 5'''\n* Werdum announces the next bout as Thiago de Lima Santos against Macario\n* Thiago Santos states that he and Macario were scheduled to fight twice before but it never happened so this is the fight he wanted.\n:'''William Macário''' defeated Thiago de Lima Santos by unanimous decision after two rounds.\n* Having regained control, Nogueira announces the fifth fight as Leonardo Santos vs. Juliano Wandalen.\n* Felipe Werdum plays a practical joke by depicting a black magic ritual in Team Nogueira's locker room, resulting in a food fight between the two teams.\n:'''Leonardo Santos''' defeated Juliano Wandalen by unanimous decision after two rounds.\n* Team Nogueira declares they are now on a comeback path after having lost the first three fights.\n\n; '''Episode 6'''\n* The show opened with a game of dodgeball between the two teams, with the right to choose the next fight on the line with Team Werdum winning.\n* For the next fight, Werdum chose Pedro Iriê to face Luiz Dutra Jr.\n* [[Antônio Rogério Nogueira|Antonio Rogerio \"Minotoro\" Nogueira]] visited his brother's team during training.\n* Dutra complained of pain in his ribs and went to the doctors, where he was cleared to fight.\n: '''Luiz Dutra Jr.''' defeated Pedro Iriê by submission (rear naked choke) in the first round.\n* Following the fight, Team Werdum's Daniel Oliveira drew the wrath of Wanderlei Silva when he stated that Dutra was a better athlete.\n\n; '''Episode 7'''\n* The final semi-finals elimination round fight is between remaining fighters Marcio Santos and Santiago Ponzinibbio.\n* Wanderlei Silva gave a speech apologizing to everyone, especially for Daniel Oliveira.\n* [[Chuck Liddell]] was a special guest who showed both teams some of his techniques.\n: '''Santiago Ponzinibbio''' defeated Marcio Santos by TKO (punches) in the first round.\n*The decision of referee Mario Yamasaki to stop the fight when Santos suffered the knockdown provoked outrage in Team Werdum. Silva accused Yamasaki of halting the fight too early.\n* White talks with the coaches over the phone to choose the next fight. Werdum picks Oliveira to face Cleiton Duarte.\n\n; '''Episode 8'''\n* Daniel Oliveira was chosen to face Cleiton Duarte.\n* Two eliminated Team Werdum fighters, Thiago Santos and Pedro Iriê, were chosen for the wild card bout for an opportunity to return to the competition.\n* Oliveira was unable to make weight for his fight and was promptly expelled from the show, allowing Duarte to advance to the next round.\n* Werdum's team opted not to coach either of his fighters in the wildcard bout.\n* '''Thiago Santos''' defeated Pedro Iriê by unanimous decision after two rounds.\n\n; '''Episode 9'''\n* The two met to plan the matchesfor the quarterfinals of the program.\n* Luiz Dutra Jr. had an MRI done for pain in his right hand. The test confirmed a torn ligament and he had to withdraw from the competition.\n* Dutra was very upset over this and cried about it, even begging to fight with one hand. Viera was then chosen to be Dutra's replacement in the quarterfinals.\n* The quarterfinals were announced:\n\n:William Macário vs. Tiago Alves\n:Leonardo Santos vs. Thiago Santos\n:David Vieira vs. Viscardi Andrade\n:Cleiton Duarte vs. Santiago Ponzinibbio\n\n*The athletes of Team Nogueira and Team Werdum engaged in a series of back-and-forth pranks.\n: '''William Macario''' defeated Tiago Alves by TKO (strikes) in the first round.\n\n; '''Episode 10'''\n* This episode featured two fights to advance to the semifinals.\n: '''Leonardo Santos''' defeated Thiago Santos by unanimous decision after two rounds.\n: '''Viscardi Andrade''' defeated David Vieira by unanimous decision after two rounds.\n* Juliano Wandalen was angered by Werdum's joke that involved putting explosives in Team Nogueira's locker room.\n* Wandalen then called his coach weak and lacking in leadership. Werdum then decides to kick him off the team, even offering him to Nogueira.\n* This was the second time for a fighter to be kicked off a team, since Andy Wang of [[The Ultimate Fighter 5|TUF 5]].\n\n; '''Episode 11'''\n* This episode featured the final quarterfinals round fight.\n* Before the fight, the coaches took part in a go-kart race for the Coaches' Challenge. Werdum won the race, earning $20,000 for his team.\n: '''Santiago Ponzinibbio''' defeated Cleiton Duarte by unanimous decision after two rounds.\n* The four semi-finalists feature three Team Nogueira fighters (William Macario, Leonardo Santos, Ponzinibbio) and one Team Werdum fighter (Viscardi Andrade).\n\n; '''Episode 12'''\n* '''William Macário''' defeated Viscardi Andrade by TKO (punches) in the third round.\n* '''Santiago Ponzinibbio''' defeated Leonardo Santos by unanimous decision after two rounds.\n* It is revealed at the end of the episode that Ponzinibbio broke his hand during the fight. Santos was chosen to replace him against Macário.\n\n==Tournament Bracket==\n{{Round16\n\n\n||{{color box|#FFCC29|border=silver}} Pedro Iriê|2|{{color box|#FFCC29|border=silver}} '''Thiago awards in three categories. The following fighters were awarded $25,000 bonuses each.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/06/tuf-brazil-2-show-bonuses-season-champ-leonardo-santos-earns-an-extra-25k|title='TUF: Brazil 2' show bonuses: Season champ Leonardo Santos earns an extra $25K|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff |date=June 9, 2013|access-date=June 9, 2013}}\n\n* '''Fight of the Season: Santiago Ponzinibbio vs. Leonardo Santos'''\n* '''Knockout of the Season: Santiago Ponzinibbio'''\n* '''Submission of the Season: Luiz Dutra Jr.'''\n\n==The Ultimate Fighter: Brazil 2 Finale==\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=UFC on Fuel TV: Nogueira vs. Werdum 2\n|image=Werdum vs Nogueira.jpg\n|promotion=[[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]\n|date= June 8, 2013\n|venue= [[Ginásio Paulo Sarasate]]\n|city= [[Fortaleza, Brazil]]\n|attendance=6,286{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/06/ufc-on-fuel-tv-10-draws-attendance-live-gate|title=UFC on FUEL TV 10 draws 6,286 attendance in Brazil|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=June 9, 2013|access-date=June 9, 2013}}\n|buyrate=\n|previousevent=[[UFC 160|UFC 160: Velasquez vs. Bigfoot 2]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC 161|UFC 161: Evans vs. Henderson]]\n}}\n'''''UFC on Fuel TV: Nogueira vs. Werdum''''' (also known as '''''UFC on Fuel TV 10''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held on June 8, 2013, at [[Ginásio Paulo Sarasate]] in [[Fortaleza, Brazil]].{{cite web|url=http://www.themmanews.com/ufc-on-fuel-tv-10-slated-for-fortaleza-brazil-antonio-rodrigo-nogueira-vs-fabricio-werdum-expected-to-headline/|title=UFC on FUEL TV 10 Slated For Fortaleza, Brazil|publisher=themmanews.com|date=March 7, 2013|access-date=March 7, 2013}}\n\n===Background===\nThe main event was a heavyweight rematch between ''The Ultimate Fighter: Brazil'' season two coaches, and perennial contenders [[Antônio Rodrigo Nogueira]] and [[Fabrício Werdum]]. It is a rematch of the first fight, in Pride, in which Nogueira won by unanimous decision back in 2006.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/03/antonio-rodrigo-nogueira-vs-fabricio-werdum-official-for-ufc-on-fuel-tv-10|title=Antonio Rodrigo Nogueira vs. Fabrício Werdum official for UFC on FUEL TV 10|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff |date=March 12, 2013|access-date=March 12, 2013}}\n\nAlso featured on the card was the welterweight finals of season two of ''The Ultimate Fighter: Brazil''.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/03/the-ultimate-fighter-brazil-2-cast-unveiled-with-28-welterweights|title='The Ultimate Fighter: Brazil 2' cast unveiled with 28 welterweights|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff |date=March 7, 2013|access-date=March 7, 2013}} The finals were originally scheduled to be [[Santiago Ponzinibbio]] versus [[William Macário]]. However, Ponzinibbio broke his hand during his semi-final fight and was replaced by semi-finalist [[Leonardo Santos (fighter)|Leonardo Santos]].\n\nA bout between [[Thiago Silva (fighter)|Thiago Silva]] and [[Rafael Cavalcante]], briefly linked to ''[[UFC 162]]'', was moved to this event to bolster the card.{{cite web|url=http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2013/3/10/4086098/report-thiago-silva-vs-rafael-cavalcante-ufc-on-fuel-tv-10-mma-news|title=Thiago Silva vs. Rafael Cavalcante moved to UFC on Fuel TV 10|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Mookie Alexander |date=March 10, 2013|access-date=March 10, 2013}}\n\n[[John Hathaway (English wrestler)|John Hathaway]] was expected to face [[Erick Silva]] at the event. However, Hathaway was pulled from the bout in late April and replaced by [[Jason High]], who was already scheduled on the card against [[Ildemar Alcantara]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/04/jason-high-replaces-john-hathaway-meets-erick-silva-at-ufc-on-fuel-tv-10|title=Jason High replaces John Hathaway, meets Erick Silva at UFC on FUEL TV 10|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff |date=April 25, 2013|access-date=April 25, 2013}} Alcantara instead faced promotional newcomer [[Leandro Silva (MMA)|Leandro Silva]].\n\n[[Derek Brunson]] was scheduled to face [[Ronny Markes]] at the event. However, the bout was scrapped on the day of the weigh in as Markes was involved in a minor traffic accident. While Markes was not seriously injured, the incident prevented him from competing.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/06/derek-brunson-vs-ronny-markes-pulled-from-ufc-on-fuel-tv-10-lineup|title=Derek Brunson vs. Ronny Markes pulled from UFC on FUEL TV 10 lineup|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff |date=June 7, 2013|access-date=June 7, 2013}}\n\nThis card broke the record for the number of submission finishes on a single UFC event. A total number of eight submission finishes occurred on the card, topping the previous total of 6 that was held by three events.{{cite web|url=http://fansided.com/2013/06/08/ufc-on-fuel-tv-10-break-record-for-most-submission-finishes/|title=UFC on FUEL TV 10 Breaks Record For Most Submission Finishes|publisher=fansided.com|author=Josh Sanchez |date=June 8, 2013|access-date=June 8, 2013}}\n\nThis was the last UFC to be branded with the ''Fuel TV'' brand; on August 17, 2013, Fox Cable Networks discontinued the ''Speed'' and ''Fuel TV'' channels, replacing them with the ''Fox Sports 1'' and ''2'' channels. The next Fox Cable UFC matches were branded as UFC Fight Night on FS1.\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card (Fuel TV)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Heavyweight\n|[[Fabrício $700,081\n|buyrate=\n|previousevent=[[UFC 162|UFC 162: Silva vs. Weidman]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC 163|UFC 163: Aldo vs. Korean Zombie]]\n}}\n'''''UFC on Fox: Johnson vs. Moraga''''' (also known as '''''UFC on Fox 8''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held on July 27, 2013, at the [[KeyArena]] in [[Seattle, Washington]].{{cite web|url=http://seattletimes.com/html/othersports/2020618710_ufcseattle23.html?hl|title=UFC announces return to KeyArena on July 27\n|publisher=seattletimes.com|date=2013-03-22|access-date=2013-03-22}}{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/03/ufc-on-fox-8-heading-to-seattles-keyarena-on-july-27|title=UFC on FOX 8 heading to Seattle's KeyArena in July|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-03-22|access-date=2013-03-22|archive-date=2023-04-04|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20230404054811/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/03/ufc-on-fox-8-heading-to-seattles-keyarena-on-july-27|url-status=dead}} The event was broadcast live on [[FX (TV channel)|FX]] and [[Fox Broadcasting Company|Fox]].\n\n==Background==\nThe main event was a [[UFC Flyweight Championship]] bout between champion [[Demetrious Johnson]] and top contender [[John Moraga]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmamania.com/2013/4/25/4264788/demetrious-johnson-vs-john-moraga-flyweight-title-fight-ufc-fox-8-seattle-mma|title=Demetrious Johnson vs. John Moraga flyweight title fight headlines UFC on FOX 8 in Seattle|publisher=MMAmania.com|author=Jesse Holland |date=2013-04-25|access-date=2013-04-25}} The pairing was originally expected to take place on April 13, 2013 at [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Jones vs. Team Sonnen#The Ultimate Fighter 17 Finale|The Ultimate Fighter 17 Finale]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/02/demetrious-johnson-defends-ufc-flyweight-title-vs-john-moraga-at-tuf-17-finale|title=Demetrious Johnson defends UFC flyweight title vs. John Moraga at TUF 17 Finale|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=February 11, 2013|access-date=April 25, 2013|archive-date=February 13, 2013|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130213084254/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/02/demetrious-johnson-defends-ufc-flyweight-title-vs-john-moraga-at-tuf-17-finale|url-status=dead}} However, Johnson was forced out of the bout with an injury and Moraga was pulled from that event as well.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/02/demetrious-johnson-injured-title-fight-with-john-moraga-off-tuf-17-finale|title=Demetrious Johnson injured, title fight with John Moraga off TUF 17 Finale|publisher=MMA Junkie|author=Staff|date=2013-02-26|access-date=2013-02-26|archive-date=2013-03-01|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130301043934/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/02/demetrious-johnson-injured-title-fight-with-john-moraga-off-tuf-17-finale|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Michael Chiesa]] was originally set to face [[Reza Madadi]] on this card.{{cite web |author=Staff |url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/04/michael-chiesa-vs-reza-madadi-added-to-ufc-on-fox-8-in-seattle |title=Michael Chiesa vs. Reza Madadi added to UFC on FOX 8 in Seattle |publisher=MMAjunkie.com |date=2013-04-23 |access-date=2013-04-23 |archive-date=2013-04-28 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130428063457/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/04/michael-chiesa-vs-reza-madadi-added-to-ufc-on-fox-8-in-seattle |url-status=dead }} However, on May 14, 2013, it was announced Madadi pulled out of the bout citing alleged visa issues and was replaced by [[Jorge Masvidal]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/05/reza-madadi-out-of-ufc-on-fox-8-jorge-masvidal-now-meets-michael-chiesa|title=Reza Madadi out of UFC on FOX 8, Jorge Masvidal now meets Michael Chiesa|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2013-05-14|access-date=2013-05-15|archive-date=2013-06-17|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130617145104/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/05/reza-madadi-out-of-ufc-on-fox-8-jorge-masvidal-now-meets-michael-chiesa|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Miesha Tate]] was pulled from her scheduled bout with [[Liz Carmouche]] to participate as coach on The Ultimate Fighter 18{{cite web|url=http://msn.foxsports.com/ufc/story/miesha-tate-replacing-cat-zingano-the-ultimate-fighter-ronda-rousey-fox-sports-1-052813|title=Tate replacing Zingano on TUF|publisher=msn.foxsports.com.com|date=2013-05-28|access-date=2013-05-28|author=A.J. Perez}} and was replaced by [[Jéssica Andrade]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2013/5/31/4385246/liz-carmouche-vs-newcomer-jessica-andrade-slated-for-ufc-on-fox-8|title=Liz Carmouche vs. newcomer Jessica Andrade slated for UFC on FOX 8|publisher=mmafighting.com|date=2013-05-31|access-date=2013-05-31|author=Ariel Helwani}}\n\n[[Tarec Saffiedine]] was expected to make his promotional debut at this event opposite [[Robbie Lawler]]; however, Saffiedine was forced out of the bout with an injury and was replaced by [[Siyar Bahadurzada]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/06/bahadurzada-replaces-injury-saffiedine-meets-lawler-at-ufc-on-fox-8|title=Bahadurzada replaces injured Saffiedine, meets Lawler at UFC on FOX 8|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2013-06-04|access-date=2013-06-04|author=Staff}} On July 11, it was announced that Bahadurzada also pulled out of the bout and was replaced by [[Bobby Voelker]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/07/siyar-bahardurzada-out-at-ufc-on-fox-8-bobby-voelker-faces-robbie-lawler|title=Siyar Bahardurzada out at UFC on FOX 8, Bobby Voelker faces Robbie Lawler|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2013-07-11|access-date=2013-07-11|author=Staff}}\n\n[[Matt Mitrione]] was expected to face [[Brendan Schaub]] at the event. However, in mid-July, Mitrione pulled out of the bout citing an injury and Schaub was pulled from the event as well with the pairing rescheduled to [[UFC 165]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2013/7/10/4512682/matt-mitrione-vs-brendan-schaub-off-ufc-on-fox-8-card|title=Matt Mitrione vs. Brendan Schaub off UFC on FOX 8 card|publisher=mmafighting.com|date=2013-07-10|access-date=2013-07-10|author=Ariel Helwani}}\n\n[[Danny Castillo]] was expected to face [[Bobby Green]] at the event, however, in mid-July, Green pulled out of the bout citing an injury and was replaced by [[Tim Means]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/07/tim-means-replaces-bobby-green-faces-danny-castillo-at-ufc-on-fox-8|title=Tim Means replaces Bobby Green, faces Danny Castillo at UFC on FOX 8|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2013-07-11|access-date=2013-07-11|author=Staff|archive-date=2013-07-13|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130713120239/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/07/tim-means-replaces-bobby-green-faces-danny-castillo-at-ufc-on-fox-8|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Yves Edwards]] was expected to fight [[Spencer Fisher]] on this card. However, on July 11, Fisher was removed from the bout due to injury and replaced by [[Daron Cruickshank]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/07/daron-cruickshank-replaces-spencer-fisher-meets-yves-edwards-at-ufc-on-fox-8|title=Daron Cruickshank replaces Spencer Fisher, meets Yves Edwards at UFC on FOX 8|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2013-07-11|access-date=2013-07-11|author=Staff|archive-date=2013-07-13|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130713120229/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/07/daron-cruickshank-replaces-spencer-fisher-meets-yves-edwards-at-ufc-on-fox-8|url-status=dead}}\n\nBoth [[John Albert (fighter)|John Albert]] and [[Tim Means]] missed weight for their respective fights; rather than use time to cut additional weight, both men forfeited 20 percent of their purses to their opponent and the bouts were made catchweight affairs.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/07/ufc-on-fox-8-live-and-official-weigh-in-results|title=UFC on FOX 8 weigh-in results: Johnson, Moraga on mark for title arts]] event held on September 21, 2013, at the [[Air Canada Centre]] in [[Toronto]], Ontario, Canada.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/05/ufc-165-heads-to-air-canada-centre-in-toronto-on-sept-21|title=UFC 165 heads to Air Canada Centre in Toronto on Sept. 21|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-05-14|access-date=2013-05-14|archive-date=2013-06-08|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130608092407/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/05/ufc-165-heads-to-air-canada-centre-in-toronto-on-sept-21|url-status=dead}} The main event was inducted into the [[UFC Hall of Fame#Fight Wing|UFC Hall of Fame's Fight Wing]] in 2021.\n\n==Background==\n\nThe main event featured a [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Jon Jones]] and [[Alexander Gustafsson]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/06/champ-jon-jones-vs-alexander-gustafsson-to-headline-ufc-165-in-toronto|title=Champ Jon Jones vs. Alexander Gustafsson to headline UFC 165 in Toronto|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Dann Stupp and Matt Erickson|date=2013-06-13|access-date=2013-06-13|archive-date=2013-06-16|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130616145723/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/06/champ-jon-jones-vs-alexander-gustafsson-to-headline-ufc-165-in-toronto|url-status=dead}}{{cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/9384346/jon-jones-set-ufc-title-defense-alexander-gustafsson|title=Jones-Gustafsson set for UFC 165|publisher=espn.go.com.com|author=Franklin McNeil|date=2013-06-13|access-date=2013-06-13}}{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/rankings|title=UFC Fighter Rankings|date=2013-06-17|access-date=2013-06-17}} Gustafsson earned his title fight by winning six straight fights in the light heavyweight division. Additionally, Jones was looking to surpass the number of Light Heavyweight title defenses originally set by [[Tito Ortiz]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/ufc/2013/4/22/4254696/jon-jones-wants-to-break-tito-ortizs-record-before-turning-to-super|title=Jon Jones wants to break Tito Ortiz's record before turning to superfights|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Dave Doyle|date=2013-04-22|access-date=2013-09-25}}\n\nCo-featured on the card was a rescheduled Interim [[UFC Bantamweight Championship|Bantamweight Championship]] bout between the current champion [[Renan Barão]], and former [[List of WEC champions#Bantamweight Championship|WEC Bantamweight Champion]] and top contender [[Eddie Wineland]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/07/renan-barao-eddie-wineland-meet-for-interim-135-title-as-ufc-165-co-main-event|title=Renan Barao, Eddie Wineland meet for interim 135 title as UFC 165 co-main event|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Matt Erickson|date=2013-07-03|access-date=2013-07-03|archive-date=2013-07-06|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130706191527/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/07/renan-barao-eddie-wineland-meet-for-interim-135-title-as-ufc-165-co-main-event|url-status=dead}} The pairing was first planned as the headliner at [[UFC 161]], but was removed from that event after Barão injured his foot.{{cite web|url=https://thestarphoenix.com/sports/mma/Barao+Wineland+interim+title+bout+Toronto/8613503/story.html|title=Barao, Wineland interim title bout set for UFC 165 in Toronto|publisher=thestarphoenix.com|author=Dave Deibert|date=2013-07-03|access-date=2013-07-03|archive-date=2013-09-25|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130925185705/http://www.thestarphoenix.com/sports/mma/Barao+Wineland+interim+title+bout+Toronto/8613503/story.html|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Mark Bocek]] was expected to face [[Michel Prazeres]] at this event. However, Bocek pulled out due to an injury and was replaced by UFC newcomer [[Jesse Ronson]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/08/newcomer-jesse-ronson-in-for-injured-mark-bocek-vs-michel-prazeres-at-ufc-165|title=Newcomer Jesse Ronson in for injured Mark Bocek vs. Michel Prazeres at UFC 165|publisher=MMAJunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-08-22|access-date=2013-08-22|archive-date=2013-08-22|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130822200644/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/08/newcomer-jesse-ronson-in-for-injured-mark-bocek-vs-michel-prazeres-at-ufc-165|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Norifumi Yamamoto]] was expected to face [[Ivan Menjivar]] at the event. However, Yamamoto was removed from the bout and was replaced by [[Wilson Reis]].{{cite web|url=http://mmadiehards.com/live/features/wilson-reis-replaces-kid-yamamoto-faces-ivan-menjivar-at-ufc-165/|title=Wilson Reis Replaces \"Kid\" Yamamoto, Faces Ivan Menjivar at UFC 165|publisher=MMAdiehards.com|author=Cameron Gidari|date=2013-09-02|access-date=2013-09-02}}\n\nThe event also aired October 17, 2013, on [[Fox Sports 1]] less than a month after it took place as part of a special UFC Reloaded.{{cite web|url=http://msn.foxsports.com/ufc/story/jones-gustafsson-airs-on-reloaded-101513|title=Jones-Gustafsson hits UFC Reloaded|work=Foxsports.com|author=Staff|date=2013-10-15|access-date=2013-10-15}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Light Heavyweight\n|[[Jon Jones]] $1,534,000\n|buyrate=\n|previousevent=[[UFC 163|UFC 163: Aldo vs. Korean Zombie]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC Fight Night: Condit vs. Kampmann 2]]\n}}\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Shogun vs. Sonnen''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 26''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held on August 17, 2013, at the [[TD Garden]] in [[Boston]].{{cite web|url=http://msn.foxsports.com/ufc/story/ufc-fox-sports-1-boston-announcement-dana-white-032913|title=UFC in Boston for FOX Sports 1|publisher=msn.foxsports.com|author=Staff|date=2013-03-29|access-date=2013-03-29}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was the first [[Ultimate Fighting Championship|UFC]] event broadcast on [[Fox Sports 1]].{{cite web|url=http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2013/3/29/4161194/dana-white-ufc-on-fox-sports-1-td-garden-boston-mma-news|title=Dana White announces UFC on Fox Sports 1 August show will be in Boston|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Mookie Alexander|date=2013-03-29|access-date=2013-03-29|archive-date=2013-04-01|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130401005751/http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2013/3/29/4161194/dana-white-ufc-on-fox-sports-1-td-garden-boston-mma-news|url-status=dead}}{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/06/ufc-on-fox-sports-1-1-six-fight-main-card-set-prelims-also-air-on-new-channe|title=UFC on FOX Sports 1 1 to feature five-hour TV run on same channel|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=2013-06-05|access-date=2013-06-05}}{{Dead link|date=January 2026 |bot=InternetArchiveBot }}\n\nThe main event was contested between former [[UFC Light Heavyweight Champion]] [[Maurício Rua]] and [[Chael Sonnen]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmamania.com/2013/6/4/4397312/chael-sonnen-vs-shogun-rua-fight-to-serve-as-first-ever-ufc-on-fox-1-main-event-boston-mma|title=Chael Sonnen vs Harvey Mitchell fight to serve as first-ever UFC on FOX Sports 1 main event on Aug. 17 in Boston|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2013-06-04|access-date=2013-06-04}} The bout was first linked to [[UFC 161]], as Sonnen was mentioned as a short notice replacement for Rua's original opponent [[Antônio Rogério Nogueira]], who was out of the bout with a back injury. Visa issues prevented Sonnen from getting into Canada, resulting in Rua being pulled from the event altogether.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/06/visa-issues-reportedly-scrap-sonnen-rua-at-ufc-161-jimmo-pokrajac-head-to-main|title=Visa issues reportedly scrap Sonnen-Rua at UFC 161|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-06-03|access-date=2013-06-03|archive-date=2013-06-08|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130608002625/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/06/visa-issues-reportedly-scrap-sonnen-rua-at-ufc-161-jimmo-pokrajac-head-to-main|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Nick Ring]] was expected to face [[Uriah Hall]] at the event. However, Ring was pulled from the bout and was replaced by [[Josh Samman]]{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/07/nick-ring-out-josh-samman-meets-uriah-hall-at-ufc-on-fox-sports-1-1|title=Nick Ring out, Josh Samman meets Uriah Hall at UFC on FOX Sports 1 1|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-07-01|access-date=2013-07-01|archive-date=2013-07-04|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130704181921/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/07/nick-ring-out-josh-samman-meets-uriah-hall-at-ufc-on-fox-sports-1-1|url-status=dead}} Samman was subsequently forced from the bout and was replaced by returning UFC fighter [[John Howard (fighter)|John Howard]].{{cite web|url=http://bostonherald.com/sports/other/inside_the_octagon/2013/07/john_howard_re_signs_with_the_ufc_fights_uriah_hall_at_ufc|title=John Howard re-signs with the UFC, fights Uriah Hall at UFC on Fox Sports 1|publisher=bostonherald.com|author=Staff|date=2013-07-17|access-date=2013-07-17|archive-date=2013-07-20|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130720020917/http://bostonherald.com/sports/other/inside_the_octagon/2013/07/john_howard_re_signs_with_the_ufc_fights_uriah_hall_at_ufc|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Akira Corassani]] was expected to face [[Mike Brown (fighter)|Mike Brown]] at the event; however, Corassani pulled out of the bout and was replaced by [[Steven Siler]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/07/akira-corassani-off-ufc-on-fox-sports-1-1-card-steven-siler-meets-mike-brown|title=Akira Corassani off UFC on FOX Sports 1 1 card, Steven Siler meets Mike Brown|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-07-01|access-date=2013-07-01|archive-date=2013-07-05|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130705011121/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/07/akira-corassani-off-ufc-on-fox-sports-1-1-card-steven-siler-meets-mike-brown|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Thiago Alves (fighter)|Thiago Alves]] was expected to face [[Matt Brown (fighter)|Matt Brown]] at the event; however, Alves pulled out of the bout citing an injury and was replaced by [[Mike Pyle (fighter)|Mike Pyle]].{{cite web|url=http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2013/7/3/4491136/mma-news-thiago-alves-injured-mike-pyle-vs-matt-brown-ufc-on-fox-sports-1-boston|title=Thiago Alves injured, Mike Pyle Sonnen'''\n\n==Reported payout==\nThe following is the reported payout to the fighters as reported to the Massachusetts State Athletic Commission. It does not include sponsor money or \"locker room\" bonuses often given by the UFC and also do not include the UFC's traditional \"fight night\" bonuses.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/09/ufc-fight-night-26-salaries|title=UFC Fight Night 26 salaries: Overeem and Rua lead disclosed paydays|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=September 10, 2013|access-date=September 10, 2013|archive-date=September 12, 2013|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130912235838/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/09/ufc-fight-night-26-salaries|url-status=dead}}\n\n*'''Chael Sonnen:''' $100,000 (no win bonus) def. known as '''''UFC Fight Night 27''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held on August 28, 2013, at the [[Bankers Life Fieldhouse]] in [[Indianapolis, Indiana]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/05/ufc-returns-to-indianapolis-on-wednesday-aug-28-for-ufc-on-fox-sports-1-event|title=UFC returns to Indianapolis on Wednesday, Aug. 28 for UFC on FOX event|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-05-11|access-date=2013-05-11|archive-date=2020-11-12|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20201112001547/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/05/ufc-returns-to-indianapolis-on-wednesday-aug-28-for-ufc-on-fox-sports-1-event|url-status=dead}} The event was broadcast live on [[Fox Sports 1]].\n\n==Background==\nThe main event was a rematch between [[Martin Kampmann]] and former [[UFC Welterweight Championship|UFC Interim Welterweight Champion]] and former [[List of WEC champions|WEC Welterweight Champion]] [[Carlos Condit]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/05/martin-kampmann-vs-carlos-condit-ii-headlines-ufc-on-fox-sports-1-2|title=Martin Kampmann vs. Carlos Condit II headlines UFC on FOX Sports 1 2|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-05-31|access-date=2013-05-31|archive-date=2013-06-07|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130607204449/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/05/martin-kampmann-vs-carlos-condit-ii-headlines-ufc-on-fox-sports-1-2|url-status=dead}} In their first encounter at [[UFC Fight Night 18]] in 2009, Kampmann defeated Condit (who was making his promotional debut) via split decision.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2009/04/01/martin-kampmann-beats-carlos-condit-by-split-decision-at-ufc-fig|title=Martin Kampmann Beats Carlos Condit By Split Decision at UFC Fight Night|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Michael David Smith|date=2009-04-01|access-date=2013-04-01}}{{cite web|url=http://hosteddb.fightmetric.com/fights/index/887|title=UFC Fight Night 18 – WELTERWEIGHT MATCH|publisher=fightmetric.com|date=2009-04-01|access-date=2013-04-01|archive-date=2013-08-24|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130824154646/http://hosteddb.fightmetric.com/fights/index/887|url-status=dead}}\n\nA bout between promotional newcomers Nandor Guelmino and [[Derrick Lewis]] was briefly linked to this event. However, Lewis was forced out of the bout with an injury and Guelmino was removed from the card and scheduled for a later bout at [[UFC 165]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/07/lewis-guelmino-off-ufc-on-fox-sports-1-2-bowling-trujillo-meet-on-card|title=Lewis-Guelmino off UFC on FOX Sports 1 2|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-07-01|access-date=2013-07-01|archive-date=2013-07-05|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130705011202/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/07/lewis-guelmino-off-ufc-on-fox-sports-1-2-bowling-trujillo-meet-on-card|url-status=dead}}\n\nA bout between [[Bobby Voelker]] and [[James Head (fighter)|James Head]] was briefly scheduled for this event. However, on July 11, it was announced that Voelker had been brought in as a replacement for [[Siyar Bahadurzada]] and would face [[Robbie Lawler]] on July 27, 2013, at [[UFC on Fox 8]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/07/siyar-bahardurzada-out-at-ufc-on-fox-8-bobby-voelker-faces-robbie-lawler|title=Siyar Bahardurzada out at UFC on FOX 8, Bobby Voelker faces Robbie Lawler|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2013-07-11|access-date=2013-07-11|author=Staff}} [[Jason High]] stepped in to face Head as a replacement for Voelker.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/07/james-head-vs-jason-high-completes-ufc-fight-night-27s-13-bout-lineup-in-indy|title=James Head vs. Jason High completes UFC Fight Night 27's 13-bout lineup in Indy|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2013-07-18|access-date=2013-07-18|author=Staff|archive-date=2013-07-21|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130721073304/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/07/james-head-vs-jason-high-completes-ufc-fight-night-27s-13-bout-lineup-in-indy|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Paulo Thiago]] was expected to face [[Kelvin Gastelum]] at the event. However, Thiago pulled out of the bout citing a knee injury and was replaced by [[Brian Melancon]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/07/brian-melancon-replaces-paulo-thiago-meets-kevin-gastelum-at-ufc-fight-night-27|title=Brian Melancon replaces Paulo Thiago, meets Kevin Gastelum at UFC Fight Night 27|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-07-23|access-date=2013-07-23|archive-date=2013-07-26|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130726123706/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/07/brian-melancon-replaces-paulo-thiago-meets-kevin-gastelum-at-ufc-fight-night-27|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Sarah Kaufman (fighter)|Sarah Kaufman]] was expected to face [[Sara McMann]] at the event. However, McMann pulled out of the bout for undisclosed personal reasons.{{cite web|url=http://www.fighters.com/08/05/sara-mcmann-out-against-sarah-kaufman-at-ufc-fight-night|title=Sara McMann Out Against Sarah Kaufman At UFC Fight Night|publisher=fighters.com|author=John Petit|date=2013-08-05|access-date=2013-08-05|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130901181038/http://www.fighters.com/08/05/sara-mcmann-out-against-sarah-kaufman-at-ufc-fight-night|archive-date=2013-09-01}} As a result, Kaufmann was pulled from the event as well.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/08/sarah-kaufman-off-ufc-fight-night-27-card-will-be-rescheduled|title=Sarah Kaufman off UFC flyweight bout between UFC newcomers [[Dustin Ortiz]] and [[Justin Scoggins]] was briefly linked to this event, but was removed from the card.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/08/ortiz-vs-scoggins-nixed-ufc-still-targeting-another-ufc-fight-night-27-bout|title=Ortiz vs. Scoggins nixed, UFC still targeting another UFC Fight Night 27 bout between [[Raphael Assunção]] and [[T.J. Dillashaw]] was briefly linked to this event.{{Cite web|url=http://mmanytt.se/ufc/tj-dillashaw-moter-raphael-assuncao-pa-ufc-on-fox-sports-1-3/|title=TJ Dillashaw möter Raphael Assuncao på UFC on FOX Sports 1 #3|publisher=mmanytt.se|author=Christoffer Esping|date=July 12, 2013}} However, the pairing was delayed and would take place at a later event this year due to a minor medical issue for Assunção.{{Cite web|url=https://www.cagedinsider.com/ufc/raphael-assuncao-vs-t-j-dillashaw-delayed/|title=Raphael Assuncao vs. T.J. Dillashaw Delayed|publisher=cagedinsider.com|author=Kelsey Mowatt|date=July 14, 2013}}\n\n[[Godofredo Pepey]] was expected to face [[Sam Sicilia]] at the event. However, Pepey pulled out of the bout citing an injury and was replaced by [[Felipe Arantes]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmaweekly.com/felipe-arantes-steps-in-to-replace-injured-godofredo-pepey-against-sam-sicilia-at-ufc-fight-night-28?vbox=1|title=Felipe Arantes Steps in to Replace Injured Godofredo Pepey at UFC Fight Night 28 on FS1 |publisher=mmaweekly.com|author=Staff|date=2013-07-18|access-date=2013-07-18}} Then, on August 7, Sicilia also pulled out and was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Kevin Souza]], the former [[Jungle Fight]] featherweight champion.{{cite web|url=http://mmanytt.se/ufc/sam-sicilia-drar-sig-ur-matchen-med-felipe-arantes-pa-ufc-fight-night-28-edimilson-souza-ersatter/|title=Sam Sicilia drar sig ur matchen med Felipe Arantes på UFC Fight Night 28, Edimilson Souza ersätter |publisher=mmanytt.se|author=Christoffer Esping|date=2013-08-07|access-date=2013-08-07}}\n\n[[Kenny Robertson]] was expected to face João Zeferino at this event. However, Robertson pulled out of the bout citing an injury and was replaced by UFC promotional newcomer [[Elias Silvério]], the former [[Jungle Fight]] welterweight champion.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/08/kenny-robertson-out-elias-silverio-in-vs-joao-zeferino-at-ufc-fight-night-28|title=Kenny Robertson out, Elias Silverio in vs. Joao Zeferino at UFC Fight Night 28|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-08-09|access-date=2013-08-09|archive-date=2013-08-11|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130811131222/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/08/kenny-robertson-out-elias-silverio-in-vs-joao-zeferino-at-ufc-fight-night-28|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Johnny Bedford]] was expected to face [[Hugo Viana (fighter)|Hugo Viana]] at this event. However, Bedford pulled out of the bout citing an injury.{{cite web|url=http://portaldovaletudo.uol.com.br/br/noticias/item/5158-advers%C3%A1rio-de-wolverine-%C3%A9-cortado-do-ufc-em-bh|title=Adversário de Hugo Wolverine é cortado do UFC em BH (Brazilian Portuguese)|publisher=portaldovaletudo.uol.com.br/|author=Staff|date=2013-08-14|access-date=2013-08-14}}{{Dead link|date=January 2026 |bot=InternetArchiveBot }} Viana was then briefly scheduled to face UFC newcomer [[Wilson Reis]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2013/8/21/4643686/wilson-reis-replaces-injured-johny-bedford-against-hugo-viana-at-ufn|title=Wilson Reis replaces injured Johny Bedford against Hugo Viana at UFN 28|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Staff|date=2013-08-21|access-date=2013-08-21}} Then just days before the event, Viana also pulled out of the bout citing an injury and the bout was cancelled.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmamania.com/2013/8/30/4674496/breaking-hugo-viana-injured-bout-with-wilson-reis-|title=Breaking: Hugo Viana injured, bout with Wilson Reis cancelled|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Matthew Roth|date=2013-08-30|access-date=2013-08-30}}\n\n[[Marcelo Guimarães]] was expected to face [[Keith Wisniewski]] at this event. [[UFC 165|UFC 165: Jones vs. Gustafsson]]\n|followingevent= [[UFC 166|UFC 166 Velasquez vs. dos Santos III]]\n}}\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Maia vs. Shields''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 29''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held on October 9, 2013, at the [[Ginásio José Corrêa]] in [[Barueri]], [[Brazil]].{{cite web|url=https://sportv.globo.com/site/combate/noticia/2013/08/anuncio-de-barueri-sp-como-sede-do-ufc-do-dia-9-de-outubro-fica-proximo.html|title=Anúncio de Barueri-SP como sede do UFC do dia 9 de outubro fica próximo|publisher=sportv.globo.com|author=Ivan Raupp|date=2013-08-05|access-date=2013-08-05}}{{cite web|url=https://www.ufc.com/news/ufc-fight-night-fox-sports-1-oct-9|title=Maia vs. Shields Headlines October Fight Night|publisher=ufc.com|date=2013-08-20|access-date=2013-08-20}} The event was broadcast live on [[Fox Sports 1]].\n\n==Background==\nThe event was headlined by a welterweight bout between [[Demian Maia]] and [[Jake Shields]],{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2013/8/12/4615012/jake-shields-demian-maia-have-agreed-to-fight-at-ufc-fight-night-29|title=Jake Shields, Demian Maia have agreed to fight at UFC Fight Night 29 |publisher=mmafighting.com |author=Guilherme Cruz|date= August 12, 2013}} with the main card broadcast on [[Fox Sports 1]].\n\n[[Rony Jason]] was expected to face [[Jeremy Stephens]] at this event. However, Jason and Stephens both pulled out due to injuries{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/09/rony-jason-pulled-from-ufc-fight-night-29-card-against-jeremy-stephens|title=Rony Jason pulled from UFC Fight Night 29 card against Jeremy Stephens|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|author=Staff|date=15 September 2013|access-date=15 September 2013|archive-date=17 September 2013|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130917003521/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/09/rony-jason-pulled-from-ufc-fight-night-29-card-against-jeremy-stephens|url-status=dead}}{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/09/stephens-injured-pulled-from-ufc-fight-night-29-palhares-vs-pierce-promoted|title=Stephens injured, pulled from UFC Fight Night 29; Palhares vs. Pierce promoted|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-09-16|access-date=2013-09-16|archive-date=2013-09-18|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130918062302/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/09/stephens-injured-pulled-from-ufc-fight-night-29-palhares-vs-pierce-promoted|url-status=dead}} and the bout was rescheduled for [[UFC Fight Night 32]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/09/rony-jason-vs-jeremy-stephens-rescheduled-for-ufc-fight-night-32-in-november|title=Rony Jason vs. Jeremy Stephens rescheduled for UFC Fight Night 32 in November|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-09-25|access-date=2013-09-25|archive-date=2013-09-26|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130926001609/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/09/rony-jason-vs-jeremy-stephens-rescheduled-for-ufc-fight-night-32-in-november|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Rodrigo Damm]] was expected to face [[Hacran Dias]] at the event. However, the bout was scrapped just days prior to the event after Damm was sidelined due to a [[kidney stone]] attack.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/10/rodrigo-damm-hacran-dias-fight-removed-from-ufc-fight-night-29|title=Rodrigo Damm-Hacran Dias fight removed from UFC Fight Night 29|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-10-05|access-date=2013-10-05|archive-date=2013-10-05|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20131005235555/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/10/rodrigo-damm-hacran-dias-fight-removed-from-ufc-fight-night-29|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Thiago Silva (fighter)|Thiago Silva]] came in 2 lb over the 206 lb weight limit and therefore [[Matt Hamill]] received 25% of Silva's purse. Their bout took place at a catchweight of 208 lb.{{cite web |url=https://www.mmamania.com/2013/10/8/4817436/thiago-silva-misses-mark-ufc-fight-night-29-weigh-ins-forfeits-purse-matt-hamill-brazil|title=Thiago Silva misses mark at UFC Fight Night 29 weigh ins, forfeits 25 percent of show purse to Matt Hamill|publisher=MMAmania.com|author=Steve Borchardt|date=2013-10-08|access-date=2013-10-08}} Dana White stated Silva would be ineligible for any finishing bonuses, no matter the outcome of the fight, due to his missing weight.\n\nEven though he was the only fighter to win by submission on the card, [[Rousimar Palhares]] was denied the ''Submission of the Night'' bonus after holding the submission after [[Mike Pierce]] had tapped, requiring the referee to physically release him. The following day, [[Dana White]] announced that Palhares would be released from his contract and banned from working at UFC ever again.{{cite web|url=https://twitter.com/JeremySchaap/status/388403602022088704|access-date=2013-10-13|title=Rousimar Palhares is banned}} He had done the same at [[UFC 111]],{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/18482/ufc-111-suspensions-palhares-receives-disciplinary-action-riddle-out-indefinitely.mma|title=UFC 111 suspensions: Palhares receives disciplinary action, Riddle out indefinitely|publisher=MMAJunkie.com|date=2010-03-28|access-date=2010-03-28|archive-date=2012-05-25|archive-url=https://archive.today/20120525133915/http://mmajunkie.com/news/18482/ufc-111-suspensions-palhares-receives-disciplinary-action-riddle-out-indefinitely.mma|url-status=dead}} earning a 90-day suspension, and was also suspended for nine months after testing positive for elevated testosterone following his loss to [[Héctor Lombard]] at [[UFC on FX: Sotiropoulos vs. Pearson]].{{cite web |url=https://themmareport.com/2013/01/rousimar-palhares-and-joey-beltran-suspended-following-positive-drug-test-at-ufc-on-fx-6|title=Rousimar Palhares and Joey Beltran suspended gate |access-date=2013-02-27}}\n|gate= 1,350,191\n|buyrate= 450,000{{cite web |url=http://mmapayout.com/blue-book/pay-per-view/ |publisher=MMAPayout.com |date=2013-06-05 |title=Pay Per View TV: Barão vs. McDonald]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC on Fuel TV: Silva vs. Stann]]\n|promotion=[[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]}}\n\n'''''UFC 157: Rousey vs. Carmouche''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] on February 23, 2013, at the [[Honda Center]] in [[Anaheim, California]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmamania.com/2012/11/14/3643402/ufc-157-location-honda-center-anaheim-ufc-fuel-tv-7-california-mma|title=UFC 157 set for Honda Center in Anaheim|publisher=MMAmania.com|date=2012-11-14|access-date=2012-11-14|last=Haggerty|first=Kevin}}\n\n==Background==\nThe headline bout between [[Ronda Rousey]] and [[Liz Carmouche]] was the first-ever [[Women's mixed martial arts|women's fight]] in UFC history.{{cite news|title=Rousey, Carmouche have UFC date|date=2012-12-07|work=[[USA Today]]|page=3C, Sports}}{{cite web|url=http://www.ocregister.com/sports/rousey-379944-mma-newly.html|title=MMA: Rousey ready to make history in MMA|publisher=Orange County Register|last=Arias|first=Carlos|date=2012-12-06|access-date=2012-12-06}} The two fought in the newly created 135-pound bantamweight division for the [[UFC Women's Bantamweight Championship]]. Rousey was the defending champion, as she was awarded the championship at a [[UFC on Fox: Henderson vs. Diaz]] pre-event press conference on December 6, 2012, prior to which she was the [[Strikeforce Women's Bantamweight Championship|Strikeforce Women's Bantamweight Champion]].{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/The-UFC-Crowns-Its-First-Queen|title=The UFC Crowns Its First Queen|publisher=UFC|date=December 6, 2012|access-date=May 11, 2016|last=Gerbasi|first=Thomas}}\n\n[[Manny Gamburyan]] was expected to face [[Chad Mendes]] at the event; however, Gamburyan was forced out of the bout with a thumb and elbow injury.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmaweekly.com/manny-gamburyan-forced-out-of-ufc-157-chad-mendes-awaits-new-opponent|title=Manny Gamburyan Forced Out of UFC 157; Chad Mendes Awaits New Opponent |publisher=mmaweekly.com|last=Pishna|first=Ken|date=2013-02-08|access-date=2013-02-08}} Mendes was then pulled from the card as a suitable replacement could not be found on short notice.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2013/02/with-no-opponent-chad-mendes-off-next-weeks-ufc-157-card|title=With no opponent, Chad Mendes off next week's UFC 157card|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff |date=2013-02-08|access-date=2013-02-08}} As a result, the bout between [[Court McGee]] and [[Josh Neer]] was promoted to the main card.\n\nFormer SuperBrawl, ICON Sport and [[Elite Xtreme Combat|EliteXC]] middleweight champion and [[Strikeforce (mixed martial arts)|Strikeforce]] middleweight title challenger [[Robbie Lawler]] made his return to UFC since [[UFC 50]], facing [[Josh Koscheck]] in the [[List of UFC champions|welterweight]] division. This is also Lawler's first fight at welterweight since [[UFC 47]].{{Cite news |last=Arias |first=Carlos |date=December 19, 2012 |title=Koscheck vs. Lawler confirmed for UFC 157 |url=http://punch.ocregister.com/2012/12/19/mma-koscheck-vs-lawler-confirmed-for-ufc-157/21334/ |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20121224162308/http://punch.ocregister.com/2012/12/19/mma-koscheck-vs-lawler-confirmed-for-ufc-157/21334/ |archive-date=December 24, 2012 |access-date=December 19, 2012 |publisher=ocregister.com}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, [[Nah-Shon Burrell]] came into his bout with [[Yuri Villefort]] heavy, weighing in at 175.8 lb. Villefort had already agreed in advance to a [[catchweight]] bout and received a portion of Burrell's purse.{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/2013/02/ufc-157-live-and-official-weigh-in-results |title=UFC 157 weigh-in results |author=Staff |publisher=MMAjunkie.com |date=2013-02-22 |access-date=2012-02-22}}\n\n15,525 people purchased tickets to watch the event at the arena, and there were 450,000 [[pay-per-view]] buys.{{cite book |last=Straka |first=Mike |author-link=Mike Straka |date=2015 |title=Rowdy Rousey: Ronda Rousey's Fight to the Top |url=https://books.google.com/books?id=2kHlCgAAQBAJ&q=%22UFC+157%22 |location=Chicago, Illinois |publisher=[[Triumph Books]] |isbn=978-1633194441 |access-date=2016-04-26 Grice'''\n* '''Knockout of the Night: Robbie Lawler'''\n* '''Submission of the Night: Kenny Robertson'''\n\n==Reported payout==\nThe following is the reported payout to the fighters as reported to the [[California State Athletic Commission]]. It does not include sponsor money and also does not include the UFC's traditional \"fight night\" bonuses or [[Pay-Per-View]] quotas.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2013/02/ufc-157-salaries|title=UFC 157 salaries: Henderson, Machida top $1.17 million payroll|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|date=2013-02-27|access-date=2013-02-27|author=Staff}}\n* '''Ronda Rousey:''' $90,000 (includes $45,000 win bonus) def. Liz Carmouche: $12,000\n* '''Lyoto Machida:''' $200,000 (no win bonus) def. Dan Henderson: $250,000\n* '''Urijah Faber:''' $100,000 (includes $50,000 win bonus) def. Ivan Menjivar: $17,000\n* '''Court McGee:''' $40,000 (includes $20,000 win bonus) def. Josh Neer: $16,000\n* '''Robbie Lawler:''' $105,000 (includes $10,000 win bonus) def. Josh Koscheck: $78,000\n* '''Brendan Schaub:''' $36,000 (includes $18,000 win bonus) def. Lavar Johnson: $29,000\n* '''Michael Chiesa:''' $30,000 (includes $15,000 win bonus) def. Anton Kuivanen: $8,000\n* '''Dennis Bermudez:''' $20,000 (includes $10,000 win bonus) def. Matt Grice: $8,000\n* '''Sam Stout:''' $52,000 (includes $26,000 win bonus) def. Caros Fodor: $15,000\n* '''Kenny Robertson:''' $16,000 (includes $8,000 win bonus) def. Brock Jardine: $8,000\n* '''Neil Magny:''' $16,000 (includes $8,000 win bonus) def. Jon Manley: $8,000\n* '''Nah-Shon Burrell:''' $12,500 (includes $7,000 win bonus) def.
